12	# Timer
13	
14	A Timer component implements countdown or stopwatch functionality with start, pause,
15	resume, and reset controls. It ticks at a configurable interval and transitions to
16	a `Completed` state when a countdown reaches zero.
17	
18	```rust
19	/// Timer mode.
20	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
21	pub enum Mode {
22	    /// Counts down from a target duration to zero.
23	    #[default]
24	    Countdown,
25	    /// Counts up from zero indefinitely.
26	    Stopwatch,
27	}
28	```
29	
30	## 1. State Machine
31	
32	### 1.1 States
33	
34	```rust
35	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
36	pub enum State {
37	    /// Timer is not running, at initial value.
38	    Idle,
39	    /// Timer is actively ticking.
40	    Running,
41	    /// Timer is paused, preserving current value.
42	    Paused,
43	    /// Countdown reached zero (only for Countdown mode).
44	    Completed,
45	}
46	```
47	
48	### 1.2 Events
49	
50	```rust
51	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
52	pub enum Event {
53	    /// Start or resume the timer.
54	    Start,
55	    /// Pause the timer.
56	    Pause,
57	    /// Resume from paused state.
58	    Resume,
59	    /// Reset to initial value.
60	    Reset,
61	    /// Reset to initial value and immediately start.
62	    Restart,
63	    /// A single tick interval elapsed.
64	    Tick,
65	    /// Set the remaining/elapsed time directly.
66	    SetTime(u64),
67	}
68	```

77	    pub current_ms: u64,
78	    /// The target duration in milliseconds (for countdown).
79	    pub target_ms: u64,
80	    /// Tick interval in milliseconds.
81	    pub interval_ms: u32,

92	}

124	
125	### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation
126	
127	```rust
128	pub struct Machine;
129	
130	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
131	    type State = State;
132	    type Event = Event;
133	    type Context = Context;
134	    type Props = Props;
135	    type Messages = Messages;
136	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
137	
138	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
139	        let current_ms = match props.mode {
140	            Mode::Countdown => props.target_ms,
141	            Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
142	        };
143	
144	        let ids = ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id);
145	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
146	        let messages = messages.clone();
147	
148	        let state = if props.auto_start {
149	            State::Running
150	        } else {
151	            State::Idle
152	        };
153	
154	        (state, Context {
155	            current_ms,
156	            target_ms: props.target_ms,
157	            interval_ms: props.interval_ms,
158	            mode: props.mode,
159	            auto_start: props.auto_start,
160	            locale,
161	            messages,
162	            ids,
163	        })
164	    }
165	
166	    fn transition(
167	        state: &Self::State,
168	        event: &Self::Event,
169	        ctx: &Self::Context,
170	        _props: &Self::Props,
171	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
172	        match (state, event) {
173	            (State::Idle, Event::Start) => {
174	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_named_effect("timer-interval", |ctx, _props, send| {
175	                    let interval = ctx.interval_ms;
176	                    let timer = set_interval(interval, move || {
177	                        send(Event::Tick);
178	                    });
179	                    Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))
180	                }))
181	            }
182	
183	            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => {
184	                match ctx.mode {
185	                    Mode::Countdown => {
186	                        let new_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_sub(ctx.interval_ms as u64);
187	                        if new_ms == 0 {
188	                            Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed).apply(|ctx| {
189	                                ctx.current_ms = 0;
190	                            }).with_named_effect("announce", |ctx, _props, _send| {
191	                                let platform = use_platform_effects();
192	                                platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.completed_announcement)(&ctx.locale));
193	                                no_cleanup()
194	                            }))
195	                        } else {
196	                            Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
197	                                ctx.current_ms = new_ms;
198	                            }))
199	                        }
200	                    }
201	                    Mode::Stopwatch => {
202	                        let interval = ctx.interval_ms as u64;
203	                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
204	                            ctx.current_ms += interval;
205	                        }))
206	                    }
207	                }
208	            }
209	
210	            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {
211	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused))
212	            }
213	
214	            (State::Paused, Event::Resume)
215	            | (State::Paused, Event::Start) => {
216	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_named_effect("timer-interval", |ctx, _props, send| {
217	                    let interval = ctx.interval_ms;
218	                    let timer = set_interval(interval, move || {
219	                        send(Event::Tick);
220	                    });
221	                    Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))
222	                }))
223	            }
224	
225	            (_, Event::Reset) => {
226	                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {
227	                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,
228	                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
229	                };
230	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(move |ctx| {
231	                    ctx.current_ms = initial_ms;
232	                }))
233	            }
234	
235	            (_, Event::Restart) => {
236	                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {
237	                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,
238	                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
239	                };
240	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).apply(move |ctx| {
241	                    ctx.current_ms = initial_ms;
242	                }).with_named_effect("timer-interval", |ctx, _props, send| {
243	                    let interval = ctx.interval_ms;
244	                    let timer = set_interval(interval, move || {
245	                        send(Event::Tick);
246	                    });
247	                    Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))
248	                }))
249	            }
250	
251	            (_, Event::SetTime(ms)) => {
252	                let ms = *ms;
253	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
254	                    ctx.current_ms = ms;
255	                }))
256	            }
257	
258	            _ => None,
259	        }
260	    }
261	
262	    fn connect<'a>(
263	        state: &'a Self::State,
264	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
265	        props: &'a Self::Props,
266	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
267	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
268	        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
269	    }
270	}
271	```
272	
273	### 1.6 Connect / API
274	
275	```rust
276	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
277	#[scope = "timer"]
278	pub enum Part {
279	    Root,
280	    Label,
281	    Display,
282	    Progress,
283	    StartTrigger,
284	    PauseTrigger,
285	    ResetTrigger,
286	    Separator,
287	}
288	
289	pub struct Api<'a> {
290	    state: &'a State,
291	    ctx: &'a Context,
292	    props: &'a Props,
293	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
294	}
295	
296	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
297	    pub fn is_running(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Running }
298	    pub fn is_paused(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Paused }
299	    pub fn is_completed(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Completed }
300	    pub fn is_idle(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Idle }
301	    pub fn current_ms(&self) -> u64 { self.ctx.current_ms }
302	
303	    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, milliseconds.
304	    pub fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {
305	        let ms = self.ctx.current_ms;
306	        let hours = ms / 3_600_000;
307	        let minutes = (ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;
308	        let seconds = (ms % 60_000) / 1_000;
309	        let millis = ms % 1_000;
310	        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)
311	    }
312	
313	    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0] (countdown only).
314	    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {
315	        if self.ctx.target_ms == 0 { return 0.0; }
316	        match self.ctx.mode {
317	            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - (self.ctx.current_ms as f64 / self.ctx.target_ms as f64),
318	            Mode::Stopwatch => self.ctx.current_ms as f64 / self.ctx.target_ms as f64,
319	        }
320	    }
321	
322	    /// Formatted time string (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS).
323	    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {
324	        let (h, m, s, _) = self.display_time();
325	        if h > 0 {
326	            format!("{:02}:{:02}:{:02}", h, m, s)
327	        } else {
328	            format!("{:02}:{:02}", m, s)
329	        }
330	    }
331	
332	    fn state_str(&self) -> &'static str {
333	        match self.state {
334	            State::Idle => "idle",
335	            State::Running => "running",
336	            State::Paused => "paused",
337	            State::Completed => "completed",
338	        }
339	    }
340	
341	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
342	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
343	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
344	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
345	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
346	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "timer");
347	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_str());
348	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), "polite");
349	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), "true");
350	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.formatted_time());
351	        attrs
352	    }
353	
354	    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
355	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
356	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();
357	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
358	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
359	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
360	        attrs
361	    }
362	
363	    pub fn display_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
364	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
365	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Display.data_attrs();
366	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
367	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
368	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("display"));
369	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
370	        attrs
371	    }
372	
373	    pub fn progress_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
374	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
375	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Progress.data_attrs();
376	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
377	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
378	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "progressbar");
379	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow), format!("{:.0}", self.progress() * 100.0));
380	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), "0");
381	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), "100");
382	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.progress_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
383	        attrs.set_style(CssProperty::Custom("ars-timer-progress"), format!("{:.2}", self.progress()));
384	        attrs
385	    }
386	
387	    pub fn start_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
388	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
389	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::StartTrigger.data_attrs();
390	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
391	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
392	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), if self.is_paused() {
393	            (self.ctx.messages.resume_label)(&self.ctx.locale)
394	        } else {
395	            (self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)
396	        });
397	        if self.is_running() || self.is_completed() {
398	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
399	        }
400	        attrs
401	    }
402	
403	    pub fn pause_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
404	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
405	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::PauseTrigger.data_attrs();
406	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
407	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
408	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.pause_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
409	        if !self.is_running() {
410	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
411	        }
412	        attrs
413	    }
414	
415	    pub fn reset_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
416	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
417	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ResetTrigger.data_attrs();
418	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
419	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
420	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), (self.ctx.messages.reset_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
421	        if self.is_idle() {
422	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
423	        }
424	        attrs
425	    }
426	
427	    pub fn separator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
428	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
429	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Separator.data_attrs();
430	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
431	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
432	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true");
433	        attrs
434	    }
435	
436	    pub fn on_start_trigger_click(&self) {
437	        if self.is_paused() {
438	            (self.send)(Event::Resume);
439	        } else {
440	            (self.send)(Event::Start);
441	        }
442	    }
443	    pub fn on_pause_trigger_click(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Pause); }
444	    pub fn on_reset_trigger_click(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Reset); }
445	    pub fn on_restart(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Restart); }
446	}
447	
448	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
449	    type Part = Part;
450	
451	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
452	        match part {
453	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
454	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
455	            Part::Display => self.display_attrs(),
456	            Part::Progress => self.progress_attrs(),
457	            Part::StartTrigger => self.start_trigger_attrs(),
458	            Part::PauseTrigger => self.pause_trigger_attrs(),
459	            Part::ResetTrigger => self.reset_trigger_attrs(),
460	            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),
461	        }
462	    }
463	}
464	```
465	
466	## 2. Anatomy
467	
468	```text
469	Timer
470	├── Root             (required — role="timer", aria-live region)
471	├── Label            (optional — describes the timer)
472	├── Display          (required — formatted time string)
473	├── Progress         (optional — role="progressbar")
474	├── StartTrigger     (required — start/resume button)
475	├── PauseTrigger     (required — pause button)
476	├── ResetTrigger     (required — reset button)
477	└── Separator        (optional — decorative colon between time segments)
478	```
479	
480	| Part         | Element    | Key Attributes                                       |
481	| ------------ | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
482	| Root         | `<div>`    | `role="timer"`, `aria-live="polite"`, `aria-atomic`  |
483	| Label        | `<label>`  | `id` for association                                 |
484	| Display      | `<span>`   | `aria-hidden="true"` (Root handles live region)      |
485	| Progress     | `<div>`    | `role="progressbar"`, `aria-valuenow/min/max`        |
486	| StartTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` (Start/Resume), `disabled` when running |
487	| PauseTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` (Pause), `disabled` when not running    |
488	| ResetTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label` (Reset), `disabled` when idle           |
489	| Separator    | `<span>`   | `aria-hidden="true"` (decorative)                    |
490	
491	## 3. Accessibility
492	
493	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
494	
495	| Part         | Role          | Properties                                               |
496	| ------------ | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
497	| Root         | `timer`       | `aria-live="polite"`, `aria-atomic="true"`, `aria-label` |
498	| Progress     | `progressbar` | `aria-valuenow`, `aria-valuemin`, `aria-valuemax`        |
499	| StartTrigger | `button`      | `aria-label`, `disabled`                                 |
500	| PauseTrigger | `button`      | `aria-label`, `disabled`                                 |
501	| ResetTrigger | `button`      | `aria-label`, `disabled`                                 |
502	
503	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
504	
505	| Key           | Action                  |
506	| ------------- | ----------------------- |
507	| Enter / Space | Activate focused button |
508	| Tab           | Move between controls   |
509	
510	### 3.3 Screen Reader Announcements
511	
512	The Root element has `role="timer"` with `aria-live="polite"` and `aria-atomic="true"`. Time changes are announced periodically. When countdown completes, the `completed_announcement` message is announced.
513	
514	## 4. Internationalization
515	
516	### 4.1 Messages
517	
518	```rust
519	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
520	pub struct Messages {
521	    /// Start button label. Default: `"Start timer"`.
522	    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
523	    /// Pause button label. Default: `"Pause timer"`.
524	    pub pause_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
525	    /// Reset button label. Default: `"Reset timer"`.
526	    pub reset_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
527	    /// Resume button label. Default: `"Resume timer"`.
528	    pub resume_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
529	    /// Progress bar label. Default: `"Timer progress"`.
530	    pub progress_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
531	    /// Completion announcement. Default: `"Timer completed"`.
532	    pub completed_announcement: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
533	}
534	
535	impl Default for Messages {
536	    fn default() -> Self {
537	        Self {
538	            start_label: MessageFn::static_str("Start timer"),
539	            pause_label: MessageFn::static_str("Pause timer"),
540	            reset_label: MessageFn::static_str("Reset timer"),
541	            resume_label: MessageFn::static_str("Resume timer"),
542	            progress_label: MessageFn::static_str("Timer progress"),
543	            completed_announcement: MessageFn::static_str("Timer completed"),
544	        }
545	    }
546	}
547	
548	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
549	```
550	
551	| Key                            | Default (en-US)     | Purpose                    |
552	| ------------------------------ | ------------------- | -------------------------- |
553	| `timer.start_label`            | `"Start timer"`     | Start button label         |
554	| `timer.resume_label`           | `"Resume timer"`    | Resume button label        |
555	| `timer.pause_label`            | `"Pause timer"`     | Pause button label         |
556	| `timer.reset_label`            | `"Reset timer"`     | Reset button label         |
557	| `timer.progress_label`         | `"Timer progress"`  | Progress bar label         |
558	| `timer.completed_announcement` | `"Timer completed"` | Screen reader announcement |
559	
560	- **Number formatting**: Time display uses locale-aware number formatting for the digits. The colon separator may vary by locale convention.
561	- **RTL**: Timer display and button layout reverse in RTL. The progress bar fills from inline-end toward inline-start.
562	
563	## 5. Library Parity
564	
565	> Compared against: Ark UI (`Timer`).
566	
567	### 5.1 Props
568	
569	| Feature            | ars-ui                       | Ark UI                | Notes                               |
570	| ------------------ | ---------------------------- | --------------------- | ----------------------------------- |
571	| `autoStart`        | `auto_start`                 | `autoStart`           | Equivalent                          |
572	| `countdown` / mode | `mode` (Countdown/Stopwatch) | `countdown` (boolean) | Equivalent (ars-ui uses enum)       |
573	| `interval`         | `interval_ms`                | `interval`            | Equivalent                          |
574	| `startMs`          | --                           | `startMs`             | Ark can start from arbitrary offset |
575	| `targetMs`         | `target_ms`                  | `targetMs`            | Equivalent                          |
576	
577	**Gaps:** None. `startMs` is niche; ars-ui can achieve via `Event::SetTime`.
578	
579	### 5.2 Anatomy
580	
581	| Part         | ars-ui                   | Ark UI                 | Notes                                               |
582	| ------------ | ------------------------ | ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
583	| Root         | `Root` (role="timer")    | `Root`                 | Equivalent                                          |
584	| Label        | `Label`                  | --                     | ars-ui has label                                    |
585	| Display      | `Display`                | `Area` + `Item`        | Ark composes time segments; ars-ui has display part |
586	| Progress     | `Progress` (progressbar) | --                     | ars-ui has progress bar                             |
587	| StartTrigger | `StartTrigger`           | `ActionTrigger(start)` | Equivalent                                          |
588	| PauseTrigger | `PauseTrigger`           | `ActionTrigger(pause)` | Equivalent                                          |
589	| ResetTrigger | `ResetTrigger`           | `ActionTrigger(reset)` | Equivalent                                          |
590	| Separator    | `Separator`              | `Separator`            | Equivalent                                          |
591	| Control      | --                       | `Control`              | Ark has wrapper for action triggers                 |
592	| Item         | --                       | `Item` (per time unit) | Ark renders each unit separately                    |
593	
594	**Gaps:** None. Ark's `Item` per-unit rendering and `Control` wrapper are layout patterns the adapter handles.
595	
596	### 5.3 Events
597	
598	| Callback | ars-ui                        | Ark UI       | Notes      |
599	| -------- | ----------------------------- | ------------ | ---------- |
600	| Complete | `State::Completed` transition | `onComplete` | Equivalent |
601	| Tick     | `Event::Tick`                 | `onTick`     | Equivalent |
602	
603	**Gaps:** None.
604	
605	### 5.4 Features
606	
607	| Feature                  | ars-ui            | Ark UI         |
608	| ------------------------ | ----------------- | -------------- |
609	| Countdown mode           | Yes               | Yes            |
610	| Stopwatch mode           | Yes               | Yes            |
611	| Start/Pause/Resume/Reset | Yes               | Yes            |
612	| Restart (reset + start)  | Yes               | Yes            |
613	| Auto-start               | Yes               | Yes            |
614	| Progress indicator       | Yes (progressbar) | No             |
615	| Completion announcement  | Yes (aria-live)   | No             |
616	| Formatted time display   | Yes (HH:MM:SS)    | Yes (per-unit) |
617	
618	**Gaps:** None. ars-ui exceeds Ark UI with progress bar and SR announcements.
619	
620	### 5.5 Summary
621	
622	- **Overall:** Full parity, with additional accessibility features.
623	- **Divergences:** Ark UI renders time as individual `Item` parts per time unit (hours, minutes, seconds); ars-ui uses a single `Display` part with `formatted_time()`. ars-ui adds a `Progress` progressbar and screen reader completion announcement.
624	- **Recommended additions:** None.
625

## Summary Report: ars-components Specialized Module Structure

### 1. Specialized Module Organization

The `specialized` module is located at `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/` and uses a **directory-backed mod.rs pattern** where each component lives in its own subdirectory.

**Existing Specialized Components (13 total):**
- `angle_slider` - circular angle-input machine
- `clipboard` - stateful clipboard copy operation machine
- `color_area` - 2D color-picker machine
- `color_field` - color-value text input machine
- `color_picker` - complex orchestrator machine
- `color_slider` - 1D single-channel color slider machine
- `color_swatch` - stateless color-preview connect API
- `color_swatch_picker` - listbox-of-swatches machine
- `color_wheel` - circular hue-picker machine
- `contextual_help` - composition API over popover
- `file_upload` - file upload queue machine
- `qr_code` - stateless QR-matrix rendering connect API

### 2. Representative Stateful Component: Clipboard

The **clipboard** component is the best representative of a stateful specialized component with timer/interval effects. It uses:
- **State enum** with 4 states (Idle, Copying, Copied, Error)
- **Event enum** with Copy, CopySuccess, CopyError, ResetTimeout, SetValue, SetProps
- **Effect enum** with named effects: WriteText, FeedbackTimer, AnnounceCopied, AnnounceError
- **Context struct** storing bindable value, duration, disabled flag, error state, locale, and component IDs
- **Props struct** with builder methods
- **Machine impl** with transition() and on_props_changed()
- **Part enum** with ComponentPart derive
- **Api struct** implementing ConnectApi
- **PendingEffect** instances with named effects and cleanup callbacks

**Key aspects:**
- FeedbackTimer effect dispatches ResetTimeout after feedback_duration_ms
- Effect cancellation: `.cancel_effect(Effect::FeedbackTimer)` when re-triggering
- no_cleanup() function for effects that don't require cleanup

**Complete file:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` (1310 lines, shown above in full)

### 5. Typical Component File Structure (from clipboard template)

A stateful component mod.rs follows this structure:

1. **Imports** (with careful use of alloc for no_std support)
2. **Type definitions**: Error enums, Status enums, etc.
3. **State enum** - Public, derives Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Display
4. **Event enum** - Public, derives Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq
5. **Context struct** - Public, stores machine state variables
6. **Props struct** - Public, derives Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId
7. **Messages struct** - Public, implements ComponentMessages
8. **Effect enum** - Public, derives Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash
9. **Machine struct** - Implements ars_core::Machine trait with:
   - init() - creates initial state and context
   - transition() - returns TransitionPlan or None
   - on_props_changed() - maps prop changes to events
   - connect() - creates Api instance
10. **Part enum** - Derives ComponentPart with #[scope = "..."]
11. **Api struct** - Implements ConnectApi with:
    - Query methods (is_idle, is_running, etc.)
    - Action methods (start, stop, etc.)
    - Attribute generation methods (*_attrs())
12. **Helper functions** - PendingEffect builders
13. **Tests module** - Comprehensive snapshot and state machine tests

### Key Patterns to Follow for Timer Component

From clipboard analysis:

**Effect patterns:**
```rust
PendingEffect::named(Effect::StartTimer)  // Effect with name only
PendingEffect::new(Effect::WriteText, |ctx, props, send| {
    // effect handler closure
    no_cleanup()  // or your cleanup function
})
```

**Transition patterns:**
```rust
TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)
    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| { ctx.elapsed = 0; })
    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::StartTimer))
    .cancel_effect(Effect::CountdownTimer)
```

**Props builder pattern:**
```rust
impl Props {
    pub fn new() -> Self { Self::default() }
    #[must_use] pub fn duration_ms(mut self, ms: u32) -> Self { /* ... */ }
}
```

**Api implementation:**
```rust
impl Api<'_> {
    #[must_use] pub const fn is_running(&self) -> bool { /* ... */ }
    pub fn start(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Start); }
    #[must_use] pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { /* ... */ }
}
impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> { /* dispatches to *_attrs methods */ }
```

## Comprehensive API Verification Report

Based on systematic code search across the ars-ui codebase, here are the exact signatures and file paths for all APIs referenced in the Timer spec:

### 1. **`ars_core::Machine` trait** ✓ EXISTS
**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs` (line 751)

**Exact trait definition:**
```rust
pub trait Machine: Sized + 'static {
    type State: Clone + Debug + PartialEq;
    type Event: Clone + Debug;
    type Context: Clone + Debug;
    type Props: Clone + PartialEq + HasId;
    type Messages: ComponentMessages + Clone + Default + 'static;
    type Effect: EffectName;
    type Api<'a>: ConnectApi where Self: 'a;

    fn init(
        props: &Self::Props,
        env: &Env,
        messages: &Self::Messages,
    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context);

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        context: &Self::Context,
        props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>;

    fn connect<'a>(
        state: &'a Self::State,
        context: &'a Self::Context,
        props: &'a Self::Props,
        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
    ) -> Self::Api<'a>;

    fn on_props_changed(_old: &Self::Props, _new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> { Vec::new() }

    fn initial_effects(
        _state: &Self::State,
        _context: &Self::Context,
        _props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> { Vec::new() }
}
```

### 2. **`TransitionPlan<M>` struct** ✓ EXISTS
**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs` (line 315)

**Key methods:**
```rust
pub fn to(state: M::State) -> Self  // Transition to new state
pub fn new() -> Self  // Create empty plan (context-only start)
pub fn apply(self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut M::Context) + 'static) -> Self  // Add context mutation
pub fn context_only(f: impl FnOnce(&mut M::Context) + 'static) -> Self  // Context-only plan
pub fn with_named_effect(
    self,
    name: M::Effect,
    setup: impl FnOnce(&M::Context, &M::Props, WeakSend<M::Event>) -> CleanupFn + 'static,
) -> Self
```

**Effect closure signature:** The closure receives:
- `&M::Context` — current context snapshot
- `&M::Props` — current props snapshot  
- `WeakSend<M::Event>` — weak event sender to avoid retain cycles
- Returns: `CleanupFn` (a `Box<dyn FnOnce()>`)

### 3. **`set_interval` and `cancel_interval`** ❌ DO NOT EXIST
**Status:** **NOT FOUND** in the codebase

**What exists instead:** 
- `PlatformEffects::set_timeout()` — single-shot timer (line 56 in platform.rs)
- No recurring interval API exists in `PlatformEffects`

**The spec's timer implementation (line 176-179) calls:**
```rust
let timer = set_interval(interval, move || {
    send(Event::Tick);
});
Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))
```

**PROBLEM:** These functions (`set_interval`/`cancel_interval`) are undefined. The spec expects an interval API that does not exist in the codebase.

**How timers actually work in Toast component** (reference implementation):
- Uses `PendingEffect::named(Effect::DurationTimer)` marker-only effect
- Adapters implement the actual timer logic based on the effect name
- The machine emits `Effect::DurationTimer` to signal "run a timer"; adapters translate that to `set_timeout` calls
- No recurring interval mechanism exists in core

### 17. **`AriaRole` enum** ✓ EXISTS (in separate crate)
**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-a11y/src/aria/role.rs` (line 18)

**Note:** AriaRole is in `ars_a11y` crate, NOT `ars_core`. The timer spec doesn't reference it directly — instead it uses `HtmlAttr::Role` with a string value like `"timer"`.

---

## CRITICAL FINDINGS

### **API MISMATCH: `set_interval` and `cancel_interval` do not exist**

The Timer spec (lines 176-222) assumes two functions that are **completely missing** from the codebase:

```rust
// SPEC (lines 176-179) — BROKEN
let timer = set_interval(interval, move || {
    send(Event::Tick);
});
Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))
```

**Reality:** 
- No `set_interval()` function exists anywhere
- No `cancel_interval()` function exists anywhere
- `PlatformEffects` only provides `set_timeout()` (single-shot) and `clear_timeout()`, not recurring intervals
- The Toast component (the only timer-like component in the codebase) handles its duration timer via marker-only effects (`Effect::DurationTimer`) — the adapter implements the actual timer logic

**Recommendation for Timer implementation:**
Instead of calling non-existent `set_interval`/`cancel_interval`, use the pattern established by Toast:
1. Emit `PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerTick)` effect to signal "start a recurring timer"
2. Let the adapter (ars-leptos, ars-dioxus) implement the actual `setInterval` call
3. The effect closure should just return `no_cleanup()` — adapters manage the timer lifecycle
4. Cancel via `plan.cancel_effect(Effect::TimerTick)` when pausing/resetting

All other APIs referenced in the spec exist with matching or compatible signatures.
```

### 1. **Unit Tests Location & Structure** (Inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`)

**Location:** Tests live inline in the component's module file using `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` at the end of the file.

**Files examined:**
- `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/overlay/dialog/mod.rs` (line 1117 onward)
- `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/input/checkbox/mod.rs` (line 817 onward)
- `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/utility/toggle/mod.rs` (line 422 onward)

**Standard imports for unit tests:**
```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use alloc::{
        rc::Rc,
        string::{String, ToString},
        vec::Vec,
    };
    use core::cell::RefCell;
    use ars_core::{Machine as MachineTrait, MessageFn, SendResult, Service};

```

**Standard test fixtures pattern:**
```rust

    Props {
        id: "timer".to_string(),  // Your component name
        ..Props::default()
    }
}

fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
    format!("{attrs:#?}")
}

**State machine transitions example (Dialog):**
```rust
#[test]
fn event_open_transitions_closed_to_open() {
    let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());
    let result = service.send(Event::Open);
    assert!(result.state_changed);
    assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Open);
    assert!(service.context().open);
}

#[test]
fn event_toggle_from_closed_opens() {
    let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());
    drop(service.send(Event::Toggle));
    assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Open);
}

#[test]
fn event_close_in_closed_state_no_op() {
    let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());
    let result = service.send(Event::Close);
    assert!(!result.state_changed);
    assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Closed);
}
```

### 2. **Snapshot Tests: AttrMap Formatting & Organization**

**Location:** Snapshots are stored in `snapshots/` subdirectory next to the module file.

**Snapshot files path:**
- Dialog: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/overlay/dialog/snapshots/`
- Toggle: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/utility/toggle/snapshots/`

**Naming convention:** `ars_components__<category>__<component>__tests__<test_name>.snap`
- Example: `ars_components__overlay__dialog__tests__dialog_root_closed.snap`
- Example: `ars_components__utility__toggle__tests__toggle_root_off.snap`

**Helper for formatting AttrMap:**

**Snapshot test pattern:**
```rust
#[test]
fn toggle_snapshots_cover_output_branches() {
    let off = Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages);
    
    assert_snapshot!(
        "toggle_root_off",
        snapshot_attrs(&off.connect(&|_| {}).root_attrs())
    );
    
    let mut on = Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages);
    drop(on.send(Event::Toggle));
    
    assert_snapshot!(
        "toggle_root_on",
        snapshot_attrs(&on.connect(&|_| {}).root_attrs())
    );
}

use proptest::test_runner::{Config, FileFailurePersistence};

#[must_use]
pub(super) fn proptest_config() -> Config {
    let cases = std::env::var("PROPTEST_CASES")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1000);

    Config {
        cases,
        failure_persistence: Some(Box::new(FileFailurePersistence::Direct(
            "proptest-regressions/state-machines.txt",
        ))),
        ..Config::default()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum DialogStep {

    SetProps(core_dialog::Props),
}

const DIALOG_EFFECTS: &[core_dialog::Effect] = &[
    core_dialog::Effect::OpenChange,
    core_dialog::Effect::FocusInitial,
    // ... all effect constants

fn arb_dialog_props() -> impl Strategy<Value = core_dialog::Props> {
    (
        (prop::option::of(any::<bool>()), any::<bool>(), /* ... */),
        (arb_focus_target(), arb_dialog_role(), 0_u8..=10, /* ... */),
    )
        .prop_map(|(/* ... */)| {
            core_dialog::Props::new()
                .id("dialog")
                .open(open)
                .default_open(default_open)
                // ... all props
        })
}

fn arb_dialog_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = core_dialog::Event> {
    prop_oneof![
        Just(core_dialog::Event::Open),
        Just(core_dialog::Event::Close),
        Just(core_dialog::Event::Toggle),
        // ... all event variants
    ]

fn assert_dialog_state_context_invariants(
    service: &Service<core_dialog::Machine>,
) -> TestCaseResult {
    // 1. State ⇔ context invariant
    prop_assert_eq!(
        service.context().open,
        matches!(service.state(), core_dialog::State::Open)
    );
    // ... more invariants
    Ok(())
}

proptest! {

    #[test]
    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
    fn proptest_dialog_state_context_invariants_hold(
        props in arb_dialog_props(),
        steps in prop::collection::vec(arb_dialog_step(), 0..128),
    ) {
        let mut service = Service::<core_dialog::Machine>::new(
            props,
            &Env::default(),
            &core_dialog::Messages::default(),
        );

        assert_dialog_state_context_invariants(&service)?;

        for step in steps {
            match step {
                DialogStep::Send(event) => {
                    let result = service.send(event);
                    assert_dialog_send_result_invariants(event, &result, /* ... */)?;
                }
                DialogStep::SetProps(props) => {
                    drop(service.set_props(props));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

### 4. **Spec-Conformance Tests**

**Location:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/`

**Helper:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/helper.rs`

**Helper function - asserts anatomy against spec:**
```rust
pub(crate) fn assert_anatomy<P>(expected_scope: &'static str, expected_parts: &[(P, &'static str)])
where
    P: ComponentPart + core::fmt::Debug + PartialEq,
{
    assert_eq!(P::scope(), expected_scope);
    let actual = P::all();
    let actual_names = actual.iter().map(ComponentPart::name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let expected_names = expected_parts.iter().map(|(_, name)| *name).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    assert_eq!(actual_names, expected_names);
    assert_eq!(actual.len(), expected_parts.len());
    
    for (actual_part, (expected_part, expected_name)) in actual.iter().zip(expected_parts) {
        assert_eq!(actual_part.name(), *expected_name);
        assert_eq!(actual_part, expected_part);
    }
    
    if let Some((first_expected, _)) = expected_parts.first() {
        assert_eq!(&P::ROOT, first_expected);
    }
}
```

**Example spec conformance test:**
```rust
// /Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/overlay/dialog.rs

#[test]
fn dialog_anatomy_matches_spec() {
    assert_anatomy(
        "dialog",
        &[
            (dialog::Part::Root, "root"),
            (dialog::Part::Trigger, "trigger"),
            (dialog::Part::Backdrop, "backdrop"),
            (dialog::Part::Positioner, "positioner"),
            (dialog::Part::Content, "content"),
            (dialog::Part::Title, "title"),
            (dialog::Part::Description, "description"),
            (dialog::Part::CloseTrigger, "close-trigger"),
        ],
    );
}

#[test]
fn dialog_content_attrs_carry_modal_role_and_label_refs() {
    let mut service = Service::<dialog::Machine>::new(
        dialog::Props::new()
            .id("dialog")
            .default_open(true)
            .role(dialog::Role::AlertDialog),
        &Env::default(),
        &dialog::Messages::default(),
    );

    drop(service.send(dialog::Event::RegisterTitle));
    let attrs = service.connect(&|_| {}).content_attrs();
    assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some("alertdialog"));
}
```

### 5. **Service Construction Pattern**

**Standard pattern for all tests:**

```rust
let service = Service::<Machine>::new(
    props,           // Your component Props
    &Env::default(), // Global environment (locale, direction, etc.)
    &Messages::default()  // Component-specific message bundle
);

let mut service = Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages);
let result = service.send(Event::Open);  // Send event & capture result

// For snapshots:
service.connect(&|_| {}).root_attrs()    // Get AttrMap for snapshot
```

**Controlled variant example:**
```rust
let service = Service::<Machine>::new(
    Props {
        open: Some(true),
        default_open: false,
        ..test_props()
    },
    &Env::default(),
    &Messages::default(),
);
```

**With custom Env/Messages:**
```rust
let es_env = Env {
    locale: Locale::parse("es").expect("`es` is a valid BCP-47 tag"),
    ..Env::default()
};

let messages = Messages {
    close_label: MessageFn::new(|locale: &Locale| {
        if locale.to_bcp47().starts_with("es") {
            "Cerrar".to_string()
        } else {
            "Close".to_string()
        }
    }),
};

let service = Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &es_env, &messages);
```

254	/// The machine for the `Clipboard` component.
255	#[derive(Debug)]
256	pub struct Machine;
257	
258	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
259	    type State = State;
260	    type Event = Event;
261	    type Context = Context;
262	    type Props = Props;
263	    type Messages = Messages;
264	    type Effect = Effect;
265	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
266	
267	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
268	        (
269	            State::Idle,
270	            Context {
271	                value: if let Some(value) = &props.value {
272	                    Bindable::controlled(value.clone())
273	                } else {
274	                    Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_value.clone())
275	                },
276	                feedback_duration_ms: props.feedback_duration_ms,
277	                disabled: props.disabled,
278	                error: None,
279	                locale: env.locale.clone(),
280	                messages: messages.clone(),
281	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
282	            },
283	        )
284	    }
285	
286	    fn transition(
287	        state: &Self::State,
288	        event: &Self::Event,
289	        _ctx: &Self::Context,
290	        props: &Self::Props,
291	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
292	        match (state, event) {
293	            (_, Event::Copy) if props.disabled => None,
294	
295	            (State::Idle, Event::Copy) => Some(copying_plan()),
296	
297	            (State::Copied | State::Error, Event::Copy) => {
298	                Some(copying_plan().cancel_effect(Effect::FeedbackTimer))
299	            }
300	
301	            (State::Copying, Event::CopySuccess) => Some(
302	                TransitionPlan::to(State::Copied)
303	                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
304	                        ctx.error = None;
305	                    })
306	                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCopied))
307	                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::FeedbackTimer)),
308	            ),
309	
310	            (State::Copying, Event::CopyError(reason)) => {
311	                let reason = reason.clone();
312	                Some(
313	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Error)
314	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
315	                            ctx.error = Some(reason);
316	                        })
317	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceError))
318	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::FeedbackTimer)),
319	                )
320	            }
321	
322	            (State::Copied | State::Error, Event::ResetTimeout) => {
323	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
324	                    ctx.error = None;
325	                }))
326	            }
327	
328	            (_, Event::SetValue(value)) => {
329	                let value = value.clone();
330	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
331	                    if let Some(value) = value {
332	                        ctx.value.set(value.clone());
333	                        ctx.value.sync_controlled(Some(value));
334	                    } else {
335	                        ctx.value.sync_controlled(None);
336	                    }
337	                }))
338	            }
339	
340	            (_, Event::SetProps) => Some(TransitionPlan::context_only({
341	                let feedback_duration_ms = props.feedback_duration_ms;
342	                let disabled = props.disabled;
343	
344	                move |ctx: &mut Context| {
345	                    ctx.feedback_duration_ms = feedback_duration_ms;
346	                    ctx.disabled = disabled;
347	                }
348	            })),
349	
350	            _ => None,
351	        }
352	    }
353	
354	    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {
355	        assert_eq!(
356	            old.id, new.id,
357	            "clipboard::Props.id must remain stable after init"
358	        );
359	
360	        let mut events = Vec::new();
361	
362	        if old.value != new.value {
363	            events.push(Event::SetValue(new.value.clone()));
364	        }
365	
366	        if old.feedback_duration_ms != new.feedback_duration_ms || old.disabled != new.disabled {
367	            events.push(Event::SetProps);
368	        }
369	
370	        events
371	    }
372	
373	    fn connect<'a>(
374	        state: &'a Self::State,
375	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
376	        props: &'a Self::Props,
377	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
378	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
379	        Api {
380	            state,
381	            ctx,
382	            props,
383	            send,
384	        }
385	    }
386	}
387	
388	/// DOM parts of the `Clipboard` component.
389	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
390	#[scope = "clipboard"]
391	pub enum Part {
392	    /// Root wrapper element.
393	    Root,
394	
395	    /// Label describing what will be copied.
396	    Label,
397	
398	    /// Button that initiates copy.
399	    Trigger,
400	
401	    /// Decorative visual state indicator.
402	    Indicator,
403	
404	    /// Live region for copy feedback.
405	    Status,
406	
407	    /// Text display for the value being copied.
408	    ValueText,
409	}
410	
411	/// API for the `Clipboard` component.
412	pub struct Api<'a> {
413	    state: &'a State,
414	    ctx: &'a Context,
415	    props: &'a Props,
416	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
417	}
418	
419	impl Debug for Api<'_> {
420	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
421	        f.debug_struct("clipboard::Api")
422	            .field("state", self.state)
423	            .field("ctx", self.ctx)
424	            .field("props", self.props)
425	            .finish_non_exhaustive()
426	    }
427	}
428	
429	impl Api<'_> {
430	    /// Whether copy succeeded and success feedback is visible.
431	    #[must_use]
432	    pub const fn is_copied(&self) -> bool {
433	        matches!(self.state, State::Copied)
434	    }
435	
436	    /// Whether a copy operation is in progress.
437	    #[must_use]
438	    pub const fn is_copying(&self) -> bool {
439	        matches!(self.state, State::Copying)
440	    }
441	
442	    /// Whether copy failed and error feedback is visible.
443	    #[must_use]
444	    pub const fn is_error(&self) -> bool {
445	        matches!(self.state, State::Error)
446	    }
447	
448	    /// Returns the current copy error reason, if any.
449	    #[must_use]
450	    pub const fn error(&self) -> Option<&CopyFailureReason> {
451	        self.ctx.error.as_ref()
452	    }
453	
454	    /// Returns the current text to copy.
455	    #[must_use]
456	    pub fn value(&self) -> &str {
457	        self.ctx.value.get()
458	    }
459	
460	    /// Dispatches copy intent.
461	    pub fn copy(&self) {
462	        (self.send)(Event::Copy);
463	    }
464	
465	    /// Root element attributes.
466	    #[must_use]
467	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
468	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
469	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
470	
471	        attrs
472	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())
473	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
474	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
475	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_token());
476	
477	        if self.ctx.disabled {
478	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true);
479	        }
480	
481	        attrs
482	    }
483	
484	    /// Label element attributes.
485	    #[must_use]
486	    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
487	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
488	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();
489	
490	        attrs
491	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"))
492	            .set(HtmlAttr::For, self.ctx.ids.part("trigger"))
493	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
494	            .set(part_attr, part_val);
495	
496	        attrs
497	    }
498	
499	    /// Trigger button attributes.
500	    #[must_use]
501	    pub fn trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
502	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
503	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Trigger.data_attrs();
504	
505	        attrs
506	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("trigger"))
507	            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button")
508	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
509	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
510	            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.trigger_label())
511	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_token());
512	
513	        if self.ctx.disabled {
514	            attrs
515	                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true)
516	                .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), "true")
517	                .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true);
518	        }
519	
520	        attrs
521	    }
522	
523	    /// Indicator element attributes.
524	    #[must_use]
525	    pub fn indicator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
526	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
527	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Indicator.data_attrs();
528	
529	        attrs
530	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
531	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
532	            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), "true")
533	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_token());
534	
535	        attrs
536	    }
537	
538	    /// Status live-region attributes.
539	    #[must_use]
540	    pub fn status_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
541	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
542	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Status.data_attrs();
543	
544	        attrs
545	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
546	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
547	            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, "status")
548	            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), "polite")
549	            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), "true");
550	
551	        attrs
552	    }
553	
554	    /// Value text attributes.
555	    #[must_use]
556	    pub fn value_text_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
557	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
558	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ValueText.data_attrs();
559	
560	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
561	
562	        attrs
563	    }
564	
565	    /// Dispatches trigger click intent.
566	    pub fn on_trigger_click(&self) {
567	        (self.send)(Event::Copy);
568	    }
569	
570	    const fn state_token(&self) -> &'static str {
571	        match self.state {
572	            State::Idle => "idle",
573	            State::Copying => "copying",
574	            State::Copied => "copied",
575	            State::Error => "error",
576	        }
577	    }
578	
579	    fn trigger_label(&self) -> String {
580	        match self.state {
581	            State::Idle => (self.ctx.messages.trigger_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
582	            State::Copying => (self.ctx.messages.copying_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
583	            State::Copied => (self.ctx.messages.copied_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
584	            State::Error => (self.ctx.messages.error_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
585	        }
586	    }
587	}
588	
589	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
590	    type Part = Part;
591	
592	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
593	        match part {
594	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
595	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
596	            Part::Trigger => self.trigger_attrs(),
597	            Part::Indicator => self.indicator_attrs(),
598	            Part::Status => self.status_attrs(),
599	            Part::ValueText => self.value_text_attrs(),
600	        }
601	    }
602	}
603	
604	fn copying_plan() -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
605	    TransitionPlan::to(State::Copying)
606	        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
607	            ctx.error = None;
608	        })
609	        .with_effect(write_text_effect())
610	}
611	
612	fn write_text_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
613	    PendingEffect::new(Effect::WriteText, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, send| {
614	        if let Some(on_copy) = &props.on_copy {
615	            on_copy((ctx.value.get().clone(), send));
616	        } else {
617	            send.call_if_alive(Event::CopyError(CopyFailureReason::ApiUnavailable));
618	        }
619	
620	        no_cleanup()
621	    })
622	}

642	    fn test_props() -> Props {
643	        Props {
644	            id: "clip".into(),
645	            default_value: "copy me".into(),
646	            ..Props::default()
647	        }
648	    }

650	    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {
651	        format!("{attrs:#?}")
652	    }
653	
654	    fn api_for_state(state: State) -> Api<'static> {
655	        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));
656	
657	        let messages = Messages::default();
658	
659	        let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);
660	
661	        ctx.messages = messages;
662	
663	        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));
664	        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));
665	
666	        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));
667	
668	        Api {
669	            state,
670	            ctx,
671	            props,
672	            send,
673	        }
674	    }
675	
676	    fn api_for_error(reason: CopyFailureReason) -> Api<'static> {
677	        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));
678	
679	        let messages = Messages::default();
680	
681	        let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);
682	
683	        ctx.error = Some(reason);
684	
685	        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));
686	        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(State::Error));
687	        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));
688	
689	        Api {
690	            state,
691	            ctx,
692	            props,
693	            send,
694	        }
695	    }
696	
697	    fn state_after_success() -> Service<Machine> {
698	        let mut service =
699	            Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages::default());
700	
701	        drop(service.send(Event::Copy));
702	        drop(service.send(Event::CopySuccess));
703	
704	        service
705	    }
706	
707	    fn state_after_error() -> Service<Machine> {
708	        let mut service =
709	            Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages::default());
710	
711	        drop(service.send(Event::Copy));
712	        drop(service.send(Event::CopyError(CopyFailureReason::PermissionDenied)));
713	
714	        service
715	    }

717	    #[test]
718	    fn clipboard_props_default_matches_spec() {
719	        let props = Props::default();
720	
721	        assert_eq!(props.id, "");
722	        assert_eq!(props.value, None);
723	        assert_eq!(props.default_value, "");
724	        assert_eq!(props.feedback_duration_ms, 2_000);
725	        assert!(!props.disabled);
726	        assert!(props.on_copy.is_none());
727	    }
728	
729	    #[test]
730	    fn clipboard_props_builder_sets_expected_fields() {
731	        let props = Props::new()
732	            .id("clip")
733	            .value("controlled")
734	            .uncontrolled()
735	            .default_value("fallback")
736	            .feedback_duration_ms(750)
737	            .disabled(true)
738	            .on_copy(callback(|_: (String, WeakSend<Event>)| {}));
739	
740	        assert_eq!(props.id, "clip");
741	        assert_eq!(props.value, None);
742	        assert_eq!(props.default_value, "fallback");
743	        assert_eq!(props.feedback_duration_ms, 750);
744	        assert!(props.disabled);
745	        assert!(props.on_copy.is_some());
746	    }
747	
748	    #[test]
749	    fn clipboard_state_display_matches_data_state_tokens() {
750	        assert_eq!(State::Idle.to_string(), "idle");
751	        assert_eq!(State::Copying.to_string(), "copying");
752	        assert_eq!(State::Copied.to_string(), "copied");
753	        assert_eq!(State::Error.to_string(), "error");
754	    }

756	    #[test]
757	    fn clipboard_initial_state_uses_default_value() {
758	        let service = Service::<Machine>::new(test_props(), &Env::default(), &Messages::default());
759	
760	        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);
761	        assert_eq!(service.context().value.get(), "copy me");
762	        assert!(!service.context().value.is_controlled());
763	        assert_eq!(service.context().feedback_duration_ms, 2_000);
764	        assert!(!service.context().disabled);
765	        assert_eq!(service.context().error, None);
766	        assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), "clip");

773	            &Env::default(),

775	        );

777	        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);

919	        assert!(<Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(&base, &base).is_empty());
920	
921	        assert_eq!(
922	            <Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(
923	                &base,
924	                &Props {
925	                    value: Some("updated".into()),

932	        assert_eq!(
933	            <Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(
934	                &base,
935	                &Props {
936	                    feedback_duration_ms: 750,
937	                    ..base.clone()
938	                },
939	            ),
940	            vec![Event::SetProps]
941	        );
942	
943	        assert_eq!(
944	            <Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(
945	                &base,
946	                &Props {

949	                },

955	    #[test]
956	    fn clipboard_api_accessors_report_each_state_error_and_value() {
957	        let idle = api_for_state(State::Idle);
958	
959	        assert!(!idle.is_copied());
960	        assert!(!idle.is_copying());
961	        assert!(!idle.is_error());
962	        assert_eq!(idle.error(), None);
963	        assert_eq!(idle.value(), "copy me");
964	
965	        let copying = api_for_state(State::Copying);

967	        assert!(!copying.is_copied());

969	        assert!(!copying.is_error());
970	
971	        let copied = api_for_state(State::Copied);
972	
973	        assert!(copied.is_copied());
974	        assert!(!copied.is_copying());
975	        assert!(!copied.is_error());
976	
977	        let error = api_for_error(CopyFailureReason::NotSecureContext);
978	
979	        assert!(!error.is_copied());
980	        assert!(!error.is_copying());
981	        assert!(error.is_error());
982	        assert_eq!(error.error(), Some(&CopyFailureReason::NotSecureContext));
983	    }

Now I understand the established effect pattern. The spec's machine impl drifts significantly: it uses string effect names and nonexistent `set_interval`/`cancel_interval`, while the codebase uses a typed `Effect` enum with marker effects that adapters implement. Let me verify the `EffectName`/`PendingEffect` contract and check the Toast component (the other timer-like one) plus `PlatformEffects`.

    fn initial_effects(

        ctx: &Self::Context,

        let mut effects = Vec::new();

        effects.push(PendingEffect::named(announce_intent(ctx.kind)));

        if ctx.duration.is_some() {
            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DurationTimer));

  "content": "# Task #294 — Implement Timer agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #294 (Epic #227, Agnostic specialized components) asks for the framework-agnostic\ncore of a **Timer** component: a stateful machine with `Idle / Running / Paused / Completed`\nstates, countdown + stopwatch modes, a configurable tick interval, auto-start, and a `ConnectApi`\nthat emits `role=\"timer\"` with an `aria-label`, a `progressbar`, and segment/formatted-time output.\nAdapters (Leptos/Dioxus) are explicitly **out of scope**. Test tier: Unit. 3 points.\n\nThe spec lives at `spec/components/specialized/timer.md` (tier: stateful, deps:\narchitecture/accessibility/interactions). The component does not exist in code yet.\n\n### Spec drift that MUST be resolved in this PR\n\nThe spec's §1.5 machine implementation predates the workspace's current effect conventions and\nwill not compile as written. CLAUDE.md requires resolving spec/code mismatches in the same task,\nso this PR will both implement the component to current conventions **and** rewrite the drifted\nspec sections to match. Three concrete drifts:\n\n1. **String effect names** (`with_named_effect(\"timer-interval\", …)`, `\"announce\"`). Current\n   `ars_core::Machine` requires `type Effect: EffectName` — a typed enum. (`EffectName` is a\n   blanket impl over `Copy + Debug + Eq + Hash + Send + Sync + 'static`, satisfied by a plain\n   `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]` enum, exactly like `clipboard::Effect` and\n   `toast::single::Effect`.)\n2. **`set_interval` / `cancel_interval` do not exist** anywhere, and `PlatformEffects` exposes only\n   `set_timeout` (no recurring interval). The recurring tick must be a **marker effect** owned by\n   the adapter — the precedent is `toast::single::Effect::DurationTimer` and\n   `clipboard::Effect::FeedbackTimer`, where the agnostic core emits a named intent and the adapter\n   wires the real `setInterval`, dispatching `Event::Tick` each interval.\n3. **Inline `use_platform_effects().announce(...)`** inside an effect closure — `use_platform_effects`\n   is adapter-only. Completion announcement becomes a marker effect (precedent:\n   `clipboard::Effect::AnnounceCopied`, `toast::Effect::AnnouncePolite`).\n\n## Files to create / modify\n\n### New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs`\nDirectory-backed module (`timer/mod.rs` + `timer/snapshots/`), mirroring\n`crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` structure. Contents:\n\n- `pub enum Mode { Countdown (default), Stopwatch }` — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]`.\n- `pub enum State { Idle, Running, Paused, Completed }` — per spec §1.1.\n- `pub enum Event { Start, Pause, Resume, Reset, Restart, Tick, SetTime(u64) }` — per spec §1.2.\n- `pub struct Context { current_ms, target_ms, interval_ms, mode, auto_start, locale, messages, ids }` — spec §1.3.\n- `pub struct Props { id, target_ms, interval_ms, mode, auto_start }` + `Default` (target 60_000,\n  interval 1000, Countdown, no auto-start) + builder methods (`new`, `id`, `target_ms`,\n  `interval_ms`, `mode`, `auto_start`) following clipboard's `#[must_use]` builder style. `#[derive(… HasId)]`.\n- `pub struct Messages { start_label, pause_label, reset_label, resume_label, progress_label,\n  completed_announcement }` with `MessageFn::static_str` defaults (spec §4.1) + `impl ComponentMessages`.\n- **`pub enum Effect { TimerInterval, AnnounceCompleted }`** (replaces spec's string names), each\n  documented with its adapter contract:\n  - `TimerInterval` — adapter starts a recurring interval of `ctx.interval_ms` ms dispatching\n    `Event::Tick`; cancelled whenever the timer leaves `Running` (adapter no-ops if none active).\n  - `AnnounceCompleted` — adapter announces `messages.completed_announcement` into a polite live region.\n- `pub struct Machine; impl ars_core::Machine for Machine` with:\n  - `init` — current_ms = target (countdown) / 0 (stopwatch); initial state Running iff `auto_start`\n    else Idle (spec §1.5).\n  - `initial_effects` — when booted into `Running` (auto-start), emit `PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)`\n    (precedent: `toast::single::initial_effects`). This is how auto-start starts ticking without a transition.\n  - `transition` — `(Idle, Start)` → Running + `with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;\n    `(Running, Tick)` countdown: `saturating_sub(interval)`; if 0 → Completed (`apply` current_ms=0)\n    `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval).with_effect(named AnnounceCompleted)`, else `context_only` decrement;\n    `(Running, Tick)` stopwatch: `context_only` `saturating_add(interval)` (defensive vs spec's `+=`);\n    `(Running, Pause)` → Paused `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval)`;\n    `(Paused, Resume | Start)` → Running `.with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;\n    `(_, Reset)` → Idle, reset current_ms `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval)`;\n    `(_, Restart)` → Running, reset current_ms `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval).with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;\n    `(_, SetTime(ms))` → `context_only` set current_ms; else `None`.\n  - `connect` — build `Api`.\n- `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"timer\"] pub enum Part { Root, Label, Display, Progress,\n  StartTrigger, PauseTrigger, ResetTrigger, Separator }` (spec §1.6 / §2; 8 parts).\n- `pub struct Api<'a>` + manual `Debug` (like clipboard) with: query accessors (`is_running`,\n  `is_paused`, `is_completed`, `is_idle`, `current_ms`), `display_time`, `progress`,\n  `formatted_time`, private `state_str`, all `*_attrs()` methods (spec §1.6 verbatim semantics:\n  Root `role=\"timer\"` + `aria-live=polite` + `aria-atomic` + `aria-label`=formatted_time +\n  `data-ars-state`; Progress `role=\"progressbar\"` + valuenow/min/max + label + custom style;\n  triggers with state-dependent `aria-label` + `disabled`; Display/Separator `aria-hidden`),\n  click handlers (`on_start_trigger_click`, `on_pause_trigger_click`, `on_reset_trigger_click`,\n  `on_restart`), and `impl ConnectApi` dispatching `part_attrs`.\n\n### Modify: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`\nAdd `pub mod timer;` (doc-commented one-liner, alphabetical-ish with siblings).\n\n### Modify: `spec/components/specialized/timer.md` (spec sync)\nRewrite §1.2-ish effect references and §1.5 machine impl to the `Effect`-enum + marker-effect model;\nadd an `Effect` enum block + adapter-contract docs; remove `set_interval`/`cancel_interval` and the\ninline `use_platform_effects().announce()`; note auto-start emits `TimerInterval` via `initial_effects`.\nKeep §1.1/1.3/1.4/1.6 API surface, §2 anatomy, §3 a11y, §4 i18n, §5 parity unchanged in contract.\n\n### Tests (TDD — write first)\n\n- **Unit + snapshot** (inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` in `timer/mod.rs`): mirror clipboard. Cover\n  every named test from the issue — full transition path `Idle→Running→Paused→Running→Completed`;\n  countdown decrements + reaches Completed with `AnnounceCompleted`; stopwatch increments from 0;\n  configurable `interval_ms`; auto-start (boots Running + `take_initial_effects` yields\n  `TimerInterval`); `SetTime`; `Reset`/`Restart` cancel/restart the interval; props defaults/builder;\n  Api accessors + `display_time`/`progress`/`formatted_time`; effect emission/cancellation via\n  `SendResult.pending_effects` / `cancel_effects` (clipboard precedent). Snapshot `*_attrs()` for\n  every part across output-affecting states (idle/running/paused/completed), committed `.snap`\n  fixtures (`cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`). Use `Service::<Machine>::new(props,\n  &Env::default(), &Messages::default())` and the `Box::leak` `api_for_state` helper from clipboard.\n- **Spec-conformance**: add `timer_anatomy_matches_spec` to\n  `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` via `assert_anatomy(\"timer\", &[…8 parts…])`.\n- **Proptest**: add a `mod timer_proptests` to\n  `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs` (mirror `color_area_proptests`):\n  arbitrary event sequences, asserting invariants — `current_ms` never exceeds `target_ms` in\n  countdown / never goes negative; state⇔effect consistency (no `TimerInterval` active after leaving\n  Running); `#![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]`, `#[ignore = \"proptest — nightly\n  extended-proptest job\"]`.\n\n## Reused existing APIs (verified)\n- `ars_core::{Machine, TransitionPlan, PendingEffect, no_cleanup, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages,\n  ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, AriaAttr, AttrMap, CssProperty, MessageFn, Locale}`.\n- `TransitionPlan::{to, context_only, apply, with_effect, cancel_effect}`,\n  `PendingEffect::named`, `Service::{new, send, set_props, connect, take_initial_effects,\n  state, context, props}`, `SendResult.{state_changed, context_changed, pending_effects, cancel_effects}`.\n- Pattern precedents: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` (structure, tests,\n  marker `FeedbackTimer`/`AnnounceCopied`), `crates/ars-components/src/overlay/toast/single.rs`\n  (`DurationTimer` marker timer + `initial_effects` for auto-started timer).\n\n## Post-implementation gate (mandatory, per CLAUDE.md step 7)\nAfter named tests pass, run the `post-implementation-audit` skill (spec-drift, \"anything missing\"\n×2, test-coverage) and land every finding in this PR.\n\n## Verification\n1. `cargo test -p ars-components timer` (unit + snapshot) — all green, zero warnings.\n2. `cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject` — snapshots committed/clean.\n3. `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance specialized::timer` — anatomy matches.\n4. `PROPTEST_CASES=1000 cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --ignored timer` — invariants hold.\n5. `cargo xtask spec validate` — spec frontmatter still consistent after spec edits.\n6. After user approval: `cargo xci-fast`, then commit + PR to `main` with `Closes #294`,\n   `snapshot-reviewed` label (new `.snap` files), then `waiting-for-codex-review` loop.\n"
