## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #294 — Implement Timer agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #294 (Epic #227, Agnostic specialized components) asks for the framework-agnostic
core of a **Timer** component: a stateful machine with `Idle / Running / Paused / Completed`
states, countdown + stopwatch modes, a configurable tick interval, auto-start, and a `ConnectApi`
that emits `role="timer"` with an `aria-label`, a `progressbar`, and segment/formatted-time output.
Adapters (Leptos/Dioxus) are explicitly **out of scope**. Test tier: Unit. 3 points.

The spec lives at `spec/components/specialized/timer.md` (tier: stateful, deps:
architecture/accessibility/interactions). The component does not exist in code yet.

### Spec drift that MUST be resolved in this PR

The spec's §1.5 machine implementation predates the workspace's current effect conventions and
will not compile as written. CLAUDE.md requires resolving spec/code mismatches in the same task,
so this PR will both implement the component to current conventions **and** rewrite the drifted
spec sections to match. Three concrete drifts:

1. **String effect names** (`with_named_effect("timer-interval", …)`, `"announce"`). Current
   `ars_core::Machine` requires `type Effect: EffectName` — a typed enum. (`EffectName` is a
   blanket impl over `Copy + Debug + Eq + Hash + Send + Sync + 'static`, satisfied by a plain
   `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]` enum, exactly like `clipboard::Effect` and
   `toast::single::Effect`.)
2. **`set_interval` / `cancel_interval` do not exist** anywhere, and `PlatformEffects` exposes only
   `set_timeout` (no recurring interval). The recurring tick must be a **marker effect** owned by
   the adapter — the precedent is `toast::single::Effect::DurationTimer` and
   `clipboard::Effect::FeedbackTimer`, where the agnostic core emits a named intent and the adapter
   wires the real `setInterval`, dispatching `Event::Tick` each interval.
3. **Inline `use_platform_effects().announce(...)`** inside an effect closure — `use_platform_effects`
   is adapter-only. Completion announcement becomes a marker effect (precedent:
   `clipboard::Effect::AnnounceCopied`, `toast::Effect::AnnouncePolite`).

## Files to create / modify

### New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs`
Directory-backed module (`timer/mod.rs` + `timer/snapshots/`), mirroring
`crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` structure. Contents:

- `pub enum Mode { Countdown (default), Stopwatch }` — `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]`.
- `pub enum State { Idle, Running, Paused, Completed }` — per spec §1.1.
- `pub enum Event { Start, Pause, Resume, Reset, Restart, Tick, SetTime(u64) }` — per spec §1.2.
- `pub struct Context { current_ms, target_ms, interval_ms, mode, auto_start, locale, messages, ids }` — spec §1.3.
- `pub struct Props { id, target_ms, interval_ms, mode, auto_start }` + `Default` (target 60_000,
  interval 1000, Countdown, no auto-start) + builder methods (`new`, `id`, `target_ms`,
  `interval_ms`, `mode`, `auto_start`) following clipboard's `#[must_use]` builder style. `#[derive(… HasId)]`.
- `pub struct Messages { start_label, pause_label, reset_label, resume_label, progress_label,
  completed_announcement }` with `MessageFn::static_str` defaults (spec §4.1) + `impl ComponentMessages`.
- **`pub enum Effect { TimerInterval, AnnounceCompleted }`** (replaces spec's string names), each
  documented with its adapter contract:
  - `TimerInterval` — adapter starts a recurring interval of `ctx.interval_ms` ms dispatching
    `Event::Tick`; cancelled whenever the timer leaves `Running` (adapter no-ops if none active).
  - `AnnounceCompleted` — adapter announces `messages.completed_announcement` into a polite live region.
- `pub struct Machine; impl ars_core::Machine for Machine` with:
  - `init` — current_ms = target (countdown) / 0 (stopwatch); initial state Running iff `auto_start`
    else Idle (spec §1.5).
  - `initial_effects` — when booted into `Running` (auto-start), emit `PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)`
    (precedent: `toast::single::initial_effects`). This is how auto-start starts ticking without a transition.
  - `transition` — `(Idle, Start)` → Running + `with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;
    `(Running, Tick)` countdown: `saturating_sub(interval)`; if 0 → Completed (`apply` current_ms=0)
    `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval).with_effect(named AnnounceCompleted)`, else `context_only` decrement;
    `(Running, Tick)` stopwatch: `context_only` `saturating_add(interval)` (defensive vs spec's `+=`);
    `(Running, Pause)` → Paused `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval)`;
    `(Paused, Resume | Start)` → Running `.with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;
    `(_, Reset)` → Idle, reset current_ms `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval)`;
    `(_, Restart)` → Running, reset current_ms `.cancel_effect(TimerInterval).with_effect(named TimerInterval)`;
    `(_, SetTime(ms))` → `context_only` set current_ms; else `None`.
  - `connect` — build `Api`.
- `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "timer"] pub enum Part { Root, Label, Display, Progress,
  StartTrigger, PauseTrigger, ResetTrigger, Separator }` (spec §1.6 / §2; 8 parts).
- `pub struct Api<'a>` + manual `Debug` (like clipboard) with: query accessors (`is_running`,
  `is_paused`, `is_completed`, `is_idle`, `current_ms`), `display_time`, `progress`,
  `formatted_time`, private `state_str`, all `*_attrs()` methods (spec §1.6 verbatim semantics:
  Root `role="timer"` + `aria-live=polite` + `aria-atomic` + `aria-label`=formatted_time +
  `data-ars-state`; Progress `role="progressbar"` + valuenow/min/max + label + custom style;
  triggers with state-dependent `aria-label` + `disabled`; Display/Separator `aria-hidden`),
  click handlers (`on_start_trigger_click`, `on_pause_trigger_click`, `on_reset_trigger_click`,
  `on_restart`), and `impl ConnectApi` dispatching `part_attrs`.

### Modify: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`
Add `pub mod timer;` (doc-commented one-liner, alphabetical-ish with siblings).

### Modify: `spec/components/specialized/timer.md` (spec sync)
Rewrite §1.2-ish effect references and §1.5 machine impl to the `Effect`-enum + marker-effect model;
add an `Effect` enum block + adapter-contract docs; remove `set_interval`/`cancel_interval` and the
inline `use_platform_effects().announce()`; note auto-start emits `TimerInterval` via `initial_effects`.
Keep §1.1/1.3/1.4/1.6 API surface, §2 anatomy, §3 a11y, §4 i18n, §5 parity unchanged in contract.

### Tests (TDD — write first)

- **Unit + snapshot** (inline `#[cfg(test)] mod tests` in `timer/mod.rs`): mirror clipboard. Cover
  every named test from the issue — full transition path `Idle→Running→Paused→Running→Completed`;
  countdown decrements + reaches Completed with `AnnounceCompleted`; stopwatch increments from 0;
  configurable `interval_ms`; auto-start (boots Running + `take_initial_effects` yields
  `TimerInterval`); `SetTime`; `Reset`/`Restart` cancel/restart the interval; props defaults/builder;
  Api accessors + `display_time`/`progress`/`formatted_time`; effect emission/cancellation via
  `SendResult.pending_effects` / `cancel_effects` (clipboard precedent). Snapshot `*_attrs()` for
  every part across output-affecting states (idle/running/paused/completed), committed `.snap`
  fixtures (`cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject`). Use `Service::<Machine>::new(props,
  &Env::default(), &Messages::default())` and the `Box::leak` `api_for_state` helper from clipboard.
- **Spec-conformance**: add `timer_anatomy_matches_spec` to
  `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs` via `assert_anatomy("timer", &[…8 parts…])`.
- **Proptest**: add a `mod timer_proptests` to
  `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs` (mirror `color_area_proptests`):
  arbitrary event sequences, asserting invariants — `current_ms` never exceeds `target_ms` in
  countdown / never goes negative; state⇔effect consistency (no `TimerInterval` active after leaving
  Running); `#![proptest_config(crate::common::proptest_config())]`, `#[ignore = "proptest — nightly
  extended-proptest job"]`.

## Reused existing APIs (verified)
- `ars_core::{Machine, TransitionPlan, PendingEffect, no_cleanup, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages,
  ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, AriaAttr, AttrMap, CssProperty, MessageFn, Locale}`.
- `TransitionPlan::{to, context_only, apply, with_effect, cancel_effect}`,
  `PendingEffect::named`, `Service::{new, send, set_props, connect, take_initial_effects,
  state, context, props}`, `SendResult.{state_changed, context_changed, pending_effects, cancel_effects}`.
- Pattern precedents: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` (structure, tests,
  marker `FeedbackTimer`/`AnnounceCopied`), `crates/ars-components/src/overlay/toast/single.rs`
  (`DurationTimer` marker timer + `initial_effects` for auto-started timer).

## Post-implementation gate (mandatory, per CLAUDE.md step 7)
After named tests pass, run the `post-implementation-audit` skill (spec-drift, "anything missing"
×2, test-coverage) and land every finding in this PR.

## Verification
1. `cargo test -p ars-components timer` (unit + snapshot) — all green, zero warnings.
2. `cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject` — snapshots committed/clean.
3. `cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance specialized::timer` — anatomy matches.
4. `PROPTEST_CASES=1000 cargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines -- --ignored timer` — invariants hold.
5. `cargo xtask spec validate` — spec frontmatter still consistent after spec edits.
6. After user approval: `cargo xci-fast`, then commit + PR to `main` with `Closes #294`,
   `snapshot-reviewed` label (new `.snap` files), then `waiting-for-codex-review` loop.

//! Framework-agnostic component state machines for ars-ui.

#![cfg_attr(not(feature = "std"), no_std)]
#![warn(clippy::std_instead_of_core)]

extern crate alloc;

/// Data-display component machines.
pub mod data_display;

/// Overlay component machines.
pub mod overlay;

/// Input component machines.
pub mod input;

/// Date and time component machines.
pub mod date_time;

/// Layout component machines.
pub mod layout;

/// Navigation component machines.
pub mod navigation;

/// Selection component machines.
pub mod selection;

/// Specialized component machines.
pub mod specialized;

[workspace.lints.clippy]
# Correctness & safety
correctness = { level = "deny", priority = -1 }
suspicious  = { level = "deny", priority = -1 }
perf        = { level = "deny", priority = -1 }

# Style & clarity
style                              = { level = "warn", priority = -1 }
needless_pass_by_value             = "warn"
cloned_instead_of_copied           = "warn"
redundant_closure_for_method_calls = "warn"
manual_let_else                    = "warn"
uninlined_format_args              = "warn"
semicolon_if_nothing_returned      = "warn"
doc_markdown                       = "warn"
match_same_arms                    = "warn"
implicit_clone                     = "warn"
trivially_copy_pass_by_ref         = "warn"

# Pedantic picks (useful, not exhausting)
needless_continue        = "warn"
if_not_else              = "warn"
redundant_else           = "warn"
map_unwrap_or            = "warn"
ref_option               = "warn"
large_futures            = "warn"
cast_lossless            = "warn"
checked_conversions      = "warn"
missing_errors_doc       = "warn"
return_self_not_must_use = "warn"

# Restriction picks
allow_attributes     = "warn"
clone_on_ref_ptr     = "warn"
missing_const_for_fn = "warn"

[profile.release]
lto           = true
codegen-units = 1
```

All lints confirmed: `cast_lossless` is on (so `u32 as u64` → use `u64::from`), `uninlined_format_args` is on (inline simple vars), `missing_const_for_fn` is on. `cast_precision_loss` is NOT enabled so `u64 as f64` is fine. Now writing the implementation.

  "content": "//! Timer component state machine and connect API.\n//!\n//! The `Timer` implements countdown or stopwatch functionality with start,\n//! pause, resume, reset, and restart controls. It ticks at a configurable\n//! interval and transitions to a [`State::Completed`] state when a countdown\n//! reaches zero.\n//!\n//! The agnostic core only emits typed [`Effect`] intents and exposes the\n//! [`Api`] connect surface; framework adapters translate\n//! [`Effect::TimerInterval`] into a real recurring interval (e.g.\n//! `setInterval`) that dispatches [`Event::Tick`], and translate\n//! [`Effect::AnnounceCompleted`] into a live-region announcement.\n\nuse alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};\nuse core::fmt::{self, Debug};\n\nuse ars_core::{\n    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty,\n    Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan,\n};\n\n/// Timer mode.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]\npub enum Mode {\n    /// Counts down from a target duration to zero.\n    #[default]\n    Countdown,\n\n    /// Counts up from zero indefinitely.\n    Stopwatch,\n}\n\n/// The state of the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum State {\n    /// Timer is not running, at its initial value.\n    Idle,\n\n    /// Timer is actively ticking.\n    Running,\n\n    /// Timer is paused, preserving its current value.\n    Paused,\n\n    /// Countdown reached zero (only for [`Mode::Countdown`]).\n    Completed,\n}\n\n/// Events for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum Event {\n    /// Start or resume the timer.\n    Start,\n\n    /// Pause the timer.\n    Pause,\n\n    /// Resume from the paused state.\n    Resume,\n\n    /// Reset to the initial value.\n    Reset,\n\n    /// Reset to the initial value and immediately start.\n    Restart,\n\n    /// A single tick interval elapsed (dispatched by the adapter's interval).\n    Tick,\n\n    /// Set the remaining/elapsed time directly, in milliseconds.\n    SetTime(u64),\n}\n\n/// Context for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// Current time value in milliseconds. For countdown this is the remaining\n    /// time; for stopwatch this is the elapsed time.\n    pub current_ms: u64,\n\n    /// The target duration in milliseconds (for countdown).\n    pub target_ms: u64,\n\n    /// Tick interval in milliseconds.\n    pub interval_ms: u32,\n\n    /// Timer mode.\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Whether auto-start is enabled.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n\n    /// Active locale inherited from provider context.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n\n    /// Component instance IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n}\n\n/// Props for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// Component instance ID.\n    pub id: String,\n\n    /// Target duration in milliseconds (for countdown mode).\n    pub target_ms: u64,\n\n    /// Tick interval in milliseconds.\n    pub interval_ms: u32,\n\n    /// Timer mode (countdown or stopwatch).\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Auto-start when mounted.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            target_ms: 60_000,\n            interval_ms: 1000,\n            mode: Mode::Countdown,\n            auto_start: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Props {\n    /// Returns a fresh [`Props`] with every field at its [`Default`] value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`id`](Self::id).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.id = id.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`target_ms`](Self::target_ms).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn target_ms(mut self, target_ms: u64) -> Self {\n        self.target_ms = target_ms;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`interval_ms`](Self::interval_ms).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn interval_ms(mut self, interval_ms: u32) -> Self {\n        self.interval_ms = interval_ms;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`mode`](Self::mode).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn mode(mut self, mode: Mode) -> Self {\n        self.mode = mode;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`auto_start`](Self::auto_start).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn auto_start(mut self, auto_start: bool) -> Self {\n        self.auto_start = auto_start;\n        self\n    }\n}\n\n/// Messages for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Messages {\n    /// Start button label. Default: `\"Start timer\"`.\n    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Pause button label. Default: `\"Pause timer\"`.\n    pub pause_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Reset button label. Default: `\"Reset timer\"`.\n    pub reset_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Resume button label. Default: `\"Resume timer\"`.\n    pub resume_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Progress bar label. Default: `\"Timer progress\"`.\n    pub progress_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Completion announcement. Default: `\"Timer completed\"`.\n    pub completed_announcement: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Messages {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            start_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Start timer\"),\n            pause_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Pause timer\"),\n            reset_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Reset timer\"),\n            resume_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Resume timer\"),\n            progress_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Timer progress\"),\n            completed_announcement: MessageFn::static_str(\"Timer completed\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n/// Typed effect intents emitted by the timer machine.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    /// Adapter starts a recurring interval of `Context::interval_ms`\n    /// milliseconds that dispatches [`Event::Tick`] on each elapse. Emitted on\n    /// initial mount when the timer auto-starts (via\n    /// [`Machine::initial_effects`](ars_core::Machine::initial_effects)) and on\n    /// every transition into [`State::Running`]. Cancelled whenever the timer\n    /// leaves [`State::Running`] (pause, completion, reset, or restart); the\n    /// adapter no-ops the cancellation when no interval is active.\n    TimerInterval,\n\n    /// Adapter announces the [`Messages::completed_announcement`] message into a\n    /// polite `aria-live` region. Emitted on the countdown transition into\n    /// [`State::Completed`].\n    AnnounceCompleted,\n}\n\n/// The machine for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        let current_ms = match props.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => props.target_ms,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n        };\n\n        let state = if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n        };\n\n        (\n            state,\n            Context {\n                current_ms,\n                target_ms: props.target_ms,\n                interval_ms: props.interval_ms,\n                mode: props.mode,\n                auto_start: props.auto_start,\n                locale: env.locale.clone(),\n                messages: messages.clone(),\n                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            },\n        )\n    }\n\n    fn initial_effects(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        _ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {\n        let mut effects = Vec::new();\n\n        // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a\n        // transition, so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.\n        if matches!(state, State::Running) {\n            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));\n        }\n\n        effects\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        match (state, event) {\n            (State::Idle, Event::Start) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_effect(interval_effect()))\n            }\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => match ctx.mode {\n                Mode::Countdown => {\n                    let new_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_sub(u64::from(ctx.interval_ms));\n                    if new_ms == 0 {\n                        Some(\n                            TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                                    ctx.current_ms = 0;\n                                })\n                                .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                                .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCompleted)),\n                        )\n                    } else {\n                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current_ms = new_ms;\n                        }))\n                    }\n                }\n                Mode::Stopwatch => {\n                    let interval = u64::from(ctx.interval_ms);\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.current_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_add(interval);\n                    }))\n                }\n            },\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n            }\n\n            (State::Paused, Event::Resume) | (State::Paused, Event::Start) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_effect(interval_effect()))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Reset) => {\n                let initial_ms = initial_ms(ctx.mode, ctx.target_ms);\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current_ms = initial_ms;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),\n                )\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial_ms = initial_ms(ctx.mode, ctx.target_ms);\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current_ms = initial_ms;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                        .with_effect(interval_effect()),\n                )\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(ms)) => {\n                let ms = *ms;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.current_ms = ms;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// DOM parts of the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"timer\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// Root wrapper element (`role=\"timer\"`, `aria-live` region).\n    Root,\n\n    /// Label describing the timer.\n    Label,\n\n    /// Formatted time-string display.\n    Display,\n\n    /// Progress indicator (`role=\"progressbar\"`).\n    Progress,\n\n    /// Start/resume button.\n    StartTrigger,\n\n    /// Pause button.\n    PauseTrigger,\n\n    /// Reset button.\n    ResetTrigger,\n\n    /// Decorative colon between time segments.\n    Separator,\n}\n\n/// API for the `Timer` component.\npub struct Api<'a> {\n    state: &'a State,\n    ctx: &'a Context,\n    props: &'a Props,\n    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Api<'_> {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"timer::Api\")\n            .field(\"state\", self.state)\n            .field(\"ctx\", self.ctx)\n            .field(\"props\", self.props)\n            .finish_non_exhaustive()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Api<'_> {\n    /// Whether the timer is actively ticking.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_running(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Running)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the timer is paused.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_paused(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Paused)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the countdown has completed.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_completed(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Completed)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the timer is idle at its initial value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_idle(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Idle)\n    }\n\n    /// The current time value in milliseconds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn current_ms(&self) -> u64 {\n        self.ctx.current_ms\n    }\n\n    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, and milliseconds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {\n        let ms = self.ctx.current_ms;\n        let hours = ms / 3_600_000;\n        let minutes = (ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;\n        let seconds = (ms % 60_000) / 1_000;\n        let millis = ms % 1_000;\n        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)\n    }\n\n    /// Progress as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.\n    ///\n    /// For countdown this is the fraction of `target_ms` already elapsed; for\n    /// stopwatch this is the elapsed time as a fraction of `target_ms`.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target_ms == 0 {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current_ms as f64 / self.ctx.target_ms as f64;\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (`HH:MM:SS` when hours are present, else `MM:SS`).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _millis) = self.display_time();\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\"{hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        }\n    }\n\n    const fn state_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self.state {\n            State::Idle => \"idle\",\n            State::Running => \"running\",\n            State::Paused => \"paused\",\n            State::Completed => \"completed\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Root element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"timer\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_str())\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), \"polite\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), \"true\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.formatted_time());\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Label element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Display element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn display_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Display.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"display\"))\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Progress indicator attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Progress.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"progressbar\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow),\n                format!(\"{:.0}\", self.progress() * 100.0),\n            )\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), \"0\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), \"100\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.progress_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            )\n            .set_style(\n                CssProperty::Custom(\"ars-timer-progress\"),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.progress()),\n            );\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Start/resume trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::StartTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                if self.is_paused() {\n                    (self.ctx.messages.resume_label)(&self.ctx.locale)\n                } else {\n                    (self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)\n                },\n            );\n\n        if self.is_running() || self.is_completed() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Pause trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pause_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::PauseTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.pause_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if !self.is_running() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Reset trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn reset_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ResetTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.reset_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if self.is_idle() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Separator element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn separator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Separator.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches start/resume intent from the start trigger.\n    pub fn on_start_trigger_click(&self) {\n        if self.is_paused() {\n            (self.send)(Event::Resume);\n        } else {\n            (self.send)(Event::Start);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches pause intent from the pause trigger.\n    pub fn on_pause_trigger_click(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Pause);\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches reset intent from the reset trigger.\n    pub fn on_reset_trigger_click(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Reset);\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches restart intent (reset and immediately start).\n    pub fn on_restart(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Restart);\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),\n            Part::Display => self.display_attrs(),\n            Part::Progress => self.progress_attrs(),\n            Part::StartTrigger => self.start_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::PauseTrigger => self.pause_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::ResetTrigger => self.reset_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// The initial `current_ms` for a given mode and target.\nconst fn initial_ms(mode: Mode, target_ms: u64) -> u64 {\n    match mode {\n        Mode::Countdown => target_ms,\n        Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds the marker effect that starts the adapter-owned tick interval.\nfn interval_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)\n}\n"
}

{

  "new_string": "/// Builds the marker effect that starts the adapter-owned tick interval.\nfn interval_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use alloc::{\n        boxed::Box,\n        string::ToString,\n        sync::Arc,\n        vec,\n        vec::Vec,\n    };\n    use std::sync::Mutex;\n\n    use ars_core::{Machine as _, Service};\n    use insta::assert_snapshot;\n\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn test_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().id(\"timer\")\n    }\n\n    fn fresh_service(props: Props) -> Service<Machine> {\n        Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default())\n    }\n\n    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n        format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n    }\n\n    fn effect_names(result: &ars_core::SendResult<Machine>) -> Vec<Effect> {\n        result\n            .pending_effects\n            .iter()\n            .map(|effect| effect.name)\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    /// Leaks a fully-formed [`Api`] in an arbitrary state for attribute and\n    /// accessor assertions, mirroring the clipboard test helper.\n    fn api_for(state: State, current_ms: u64, mode: Mode, target_ms: u64) -> Api<'static> {\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(\n            Props::new().id(\"timer\").mode(mode).target_ms(target_ms),\n        ));\n        let messages = Messages::default();\n        let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);\n        ctx.current_ms = current_ms;\n\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));\n\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Props ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_props_default_matches_spec() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"\");\n        assert_eq!(props.target_ms, 60_000);\n        assert_eq!(props.interval_ms, 1000);\n        assert_eq!(props.mode, Mode::Countdown);\n        assert!(!props.auto_start);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_props_builder_sets_expected_fields() {\n        let props = Props::new()\n            .id(\"timer\")\n            .target_ms(5_000)\n            .interval_ms(250)\n            .mode(Mode::Stopwatch)\n            .auto_start(true);\n\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"timer\");\n        assert_eq!(props.target_ms, 5_000);\n        assert_eq!(props.interval_ms, 250);\n        assert_eq!(props.mode, Mode::Stopwatch);\n        assert!(props.auto_start);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_mode_default_is_countdown() {\n        assert_eq!(Mode::default(), Mode::Countdown);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── init ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_init_starts_idle_at_target() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(30_000));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 30_000);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().target_ms, 30_000);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().mode, Mode::Countdown);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), \"timer\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_stopwatch_init_starts_idle_at_zero() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).target_ms(30_000));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_auto_start_boots_running_and_emits_interval_effect() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().auto_start(true));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(\n            service\n                .take_initial_effects()\n                .iter()\n                .map(|effect| effect.name)\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),\n            vec![Effect::TimerInterval]\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_without_auto_start_emits_no_initial_effects() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.take_initial_effects().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_full_lifecycle_idle_running_paused_running_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(2_000).interval_ms(1_000));\n\n        let started = service.send(Event::Start);\n        assert!(started.state_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&started), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let tick = service.send(Event::Tick);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 1_000);\n        assert!(tick.pending_effects.is_empty());\n\n        let paused = service.send(Event::Pause);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Paused);\n        assert_eq!(paused.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let resumed = service.send(Event::Resume);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&resumed), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let completed = service.send(Event::Tick);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 0);\n        assert_eq!(completed.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&completed), vec![Effect::AnnounceCompleted]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_tick_decrements_by_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(10_000).interval_ms(2_500));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 7_500);\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 5_000);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_tick_at_or_below_interval_completes() {\n        // current (500) < interval (1000) saturates to 0 and completes.\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(500).interval_ms(1_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::Tick);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 0);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::AnnounceCompleted]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_stopwatch_tick_increments_from_zero() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).interval_ms(1_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 1_000);\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 2_000);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_stopwatch_never_completes() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).target_ms(1_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        for _ in 0..5 {\n            drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        }\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 5_000);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_reset_returns_to_idle_and_cancels_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(10_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::Reset);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 10_000);\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_reset_stopwatch_returns_to_zero() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Reset));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_restart_resets_value_and_restarts_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(10_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Pause));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::Restart);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 10_000);\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_time_updates_current_without_changing_state() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(10_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::SetTime(3_333));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current_ms, 3_333);\n        assert!(result.context_changed);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_ignores_unhandled_events() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        // Tick while idle is a no-op.\n        let result = service.send(Event::Tick);\n        assert!(!result.state_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n\n        // Pause while idle is a no-op.\n        let result = service.send(Event::Pause);\n        assert!(!result.state_changed);\n\n        // Start from completed is rejected.\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target_ms(1_000).interval_ms(1_000));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        let result = service.send(Event::Start);\n        assert!(!result.state_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_state_predicates_match_state() {\n        assert!(api_for(State::Idle, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).is_idle());\n        assert!(api_for(State::Running, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).is_running());\n        assert!(api_for(State::Paused, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).is_paused());\n        assert!(api_for(State::Completed, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).is_completed());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_current_ms_reports_context() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            api_for(State::Running, 4_200, Mode::Countdown, 10_000).current_ms(),\n            4_200\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_display_time_splits_segments() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Running, 3_661_000, Mode::Countdown, 3_661_000);\n        assert_eq!(api.display_time(), (1, 1, 1, 0));\n\n        let api = api_for(State::Running, 90_500, Mode::Countdown, 90_500);\n        assert_eq!(api.display_time(), (0, 1, 30, 500));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_formatted_time_uses_hours_only_when_present() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            api_for(State::Running, 3_661_000, Mode::Countdown, 3_661_000).formatted_time(),\n            \"01:01:01\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            api_for(State::Running, 90_000, Mode::Countdown, 90_000).formatted_time(),\n            \"01:30\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_progress_countdown_and_stopwatch() {\n        // Countdown: progress is the elapsed fraction.\n        assert!((api_for(State::Idle, 1_000, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).progress() - 0.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n        assert!((api_for(State::Running, 500, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).progress() - 0.5).abs() < 1e-9);\n        assert!(\n            (api_for(State::Completed, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).progress() - 1.0).abs() < 1e-9\n        );\n\n        // Stopwatch: progress is the elapsed fraction of target.\n        assert!(\n            (api_for(State::Running, 500, Mode::Stopwatch, 1_000).progress() - 0.5).abs() < 1e-9\n        );\n\n        // Zero target guards against division by zero.\n        assert!((api_for(State::Idle, 0, Mode::Countdown, 0).progress() - 0.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_root_attrs_carry_timer_role_live_region_and_label() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Running, 30_000, Mode::Countdown, 60_000);\n        let attrs = assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic)), Some(\"true\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)), Some(\"00:30\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\")), Some(\"running\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_root_data_state_tracks_each_state() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            api_for(State::Idle, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000)\n                .root_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\")),\n            Some(\"idle\")\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n      );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_progress_attrs_expose_progressbar_role_and_values() {\n        let attrs = api_for(State::Completed, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000).progress_attrs();\n\n assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin)), Some(\"0\"));\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax)), Some(\"100\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"Timer progress\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_trigger_disabled_states_match_spec() {\n        // StartTrigger disabled while running or completed.\n        assert!(\n            api_for(State::Running, 0, Mode::Countdown, 1_000)\n                .start_trigger_attrs()\n .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"Resume timer\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_click_handlers_dispatch_expected_events() {\n        let events =  fresh_service(test_props())\n            .connect(&start_from_idle)\n            .on_start_trigger_click();\n\n        // Resume path from paused.\n        let mut paused = move |event| captured.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n            paused.connect(&sink).on_start_trigger_click();\n        }\n\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        {\n            let captured = Arc::clone(&events);\n            let sink = move |event| captured.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n            let api = service.connect(&sink);\n            api.on_pause_trigger_click();\n            api.on_reset_trigger_click();\n            api.on_restart();\n        }\n\n        assert_eq!(\n            *events.lock().unwrap(),\n            vec![\n //! Property-based tests for the specialized components.
//!
//! The color-machine blocks feed an arbitrary event sequence through a machine
//! and assert the component's core invariants always hold: stored values stay
//! in range, states stay within the declared set, and `connect()` never panics.
//! The stateless `QrCode` block instead pins its prop->attr mapping across the
//! whole input space. Run in the nightly `extended-proptest` job; `#[ignore]`d
//! in the fast tier.

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ColorArea
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

mod color_area_proptests {
    use ars_components::specialized::color_area::{Event, Machine, Props};
    use ars_core::{AriaAttr, ColorChannel, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};
    use proptest::prelude::*;

    fn arb_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {
        prop_oneof![
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragStart { x, y }),
            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragMove { x, y }),
            Just(Event::DragEnd),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementX { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementX { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementY { step }),
            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementY { step }),
            Just(Event::SetXToMin),
            Just(Event::SetXToMax),
            Just(Event::SetYToMin),
            Just(Event::SetYToMax),
            any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),
            Just(Event::Blur),
        ]
    }

    proptest! {
        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]

        #[test]
        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
        fn area_event_sequences_keep_value_valid(
            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64),
        ) {
            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(
                Props { id: "ca".into(), ..Props::default() },
                &Env::default(),
                &Default::default(),
            );

            for ev in events {
                drop(svc.send(ev));
            }

            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});

            let value = *api.value();

            prop_assert!((0.0..360.0).contains(&value.hue));
            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.saturation));
            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.lightness));

            // connect output reflects the in-range state: the thumb always
            // exposes a non-empty `aria-valuetext` describing the current color.
            let thumb = api.thumb_attrs();

            prop_assert!(
                thumb
                    .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueText))
                    .is_some_and(|text| !text.is_empty())
            );

            // The two channels stay within their ranges.
            for ch in [ColorChannel::Saturation, ColorChannel::Lightness] {
                let (min, max) = ars_core::channel_range(ch);

                let val = ars_core::channel_value(&value, ch);

                prop_assert!(val >= min - 1e-9 && val <= max + 1e-9);
            }
        }
    }
}

// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ColorSlider
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

mod color_slider_proptests {
    use ars_components::specialized::color_slider::{Event, Machine, Props};
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294\ncat >> crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs <<'RUST'\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Timer\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nmod timer_proptests {\n    use ars_components::specialized::timer::{Event, Machine, Mode, Props, State};\n    use ars_core::{Env, Service};\n    use proptest::prelude::*;\n\n    fn arb_mode() -> impl Strategy<Value = Mode> {\n        prop_oneof![Just(Mode::Countdown), Just(Mode::Stopwatch)]\n    }\n\n    prop_compose! {\n        fn arb_props()(\n            mode in arb_mode(),\n            target_ms in 1_000u64..=600_000,\n            interval_ms in 1u32..=60_000,\n            auto_start in any::<bool>(),\n        ) -> Props {\n            Props::new()\n                .id(\"timer\")\n                .mode(mode)\n                .target_ms(target_ms)\n                .interval_ms(interval_ms)\n                .auto_start(auto_start)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Events parameterised by `target` so `SetTime` stays within the target\n    /// range; this keeps the \"countdown never exceeds target\" invariant true\n    /// while still exercising the `SetTime` branch.\n    fn arb_event(target: u64) -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            Just(Event::Start),\n            Just(Event::Pause),\n            Just(Event::Resume),\n            Just(Event::Reset),\n            Just(Event::Restart),\n            Just(Event::Tick),\n            (0u64..=target).prop_map(Event::SetTime),\n        ]\n    }\n\n    fn arb_scenario() -> impl Strategy<Value = (Props, Vec<Event>)> {\n        arb_props().prop_flat_map(|props| {\n            let events = prop::collection::vec(arb_event(props.target_ms), 0..128);\n            (Just(props), events)\n        })\n    }\n\n    proptest! {\n        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]\n\n        #[test]\n        #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n        fn timer_event_sequences_preserve_invariants((props, events) in arb_scenario()) {\n            let mode = props.mode;\n            let target_ms = props.target_ms;\n\n            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n            drop(svc.take_initial_effects());\n\n            for ev in events {\n                drop(svc.send(ev));\n\n                let state = *svc.state();\n                let current = svc.context().current_ms;\n\n                // Stopwatch mode can never reach the completed state.\n                if mode == Mode::Stopwatch {\n                    prop_assert_ne!(state, State::Completed);\n                }\n\n                // A completed countdown always rests at zero.\n                if state == State::Completed {\n                    prop_assert_eq!(current, 0);\n                }\n\n                // Countdown never exceeds its target (SetTime is bounded to it).\n                if mode == Mode::Countdown {\n                    prop_assert!(current <= target_ms);\n                }\n\n                // `connect()` and its output methods never panic and always\n                // produce finite progress and a non-empty formatted time.\n                let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n                prop_assert!(api.progress().is_finite());\n                prop_assert!(!api.formatted_time().is_empty());\n                let _ = api.root_attrs();\n                let _ = api.progress_attrs();\n                let _ = api.start_trigger_attrs();\n                let _ = api.pause_trigger_attrs();\n                let _ = api.reset_trigger_attrs();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\nRUST\necho \"appended\"",
  "description": "Append timer proptest module"
}

> AGENT

The proptest found a real semantic detail: `SetTime` is unconditional (`(_, SetTime)`), so it can set a nonzero `current_ms` while the machine stays in `Completed`. That's correct per-spec behavior (matches the existing `clipboard`/`toast` unconditional context-sync pattern), so my per-step invariant was too strong. The completion-at-zero guarantee is already covered by the unit test (`timer_full_lifecycle...`). Let me relax that proptest invariant.

      let locale = env.locale.clone();\n        let messages = messages.clone();\n\n        let state = if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n set_interval(interval, move || {\n                        send(Event::Tick);\n                    });\n                    Box::new(move || cancel_interval(timer))\n                }))\n            }\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => {\n                match ctx.mode {\n                    Mode::Countdown => {\n                        let new_ms = Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {\n                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,\n                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).apply(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.current_ms = initial_ms;\n                }).with_named_effect(\"timer-interval\", |ctx, _props, send| {\n state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n```",
  "new_string": "### 1.5 Effects\n\nThe recurring tick and the completion announcement are side effects the agnostic core cannot\nperform itself (it has no DOM and no recurring-interval primitive — `PlatformEffects` exposes only\n`set_timeout`). They are therefore emitted as typed **marker effects** that framework adapters\ntranslate into real platform calls, exactly like `clipboard::Effect::FeedbackTimer` and\n`toast::single::Effect::DurationTimer`.\n\n```rust\n/// Typed effect intents emitted by the timer machine.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    /// Adapter starts a recurring interval of `Context::interval_ms`\n    /// milliseconds that dispatches `Event::Tick` on each elapse. Emitted on\n    /// initial mount when the timer auto-starts (via `Machine::initial_effects`)\n    /// and on every transition into `State::Running`. Cancelled whenever the\n    /// timer leaves `State::Running` (pause, completion, reset, or restart);\n    /// the adapter no-ops the cancellation when no interval is active.\n    TimerInterval,\n\n    /// Adapter announces the `Messages::completed_announcement` message into a\n    /// polite `aria-live` region. Emitted on the countdown transition into\n    /// `State::Completed`.\n    AnnounceCompleted,\n}\n```\n\n### 1.6 Full Machine Implementation\n\n```rust\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {\n        let current_ms = match props.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => props.target_ms,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n        };\n\n        let state = if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n        };\n\n        (state, Context {\n            current_ms,\n            target_ms: props.target_ms,\n            interval_ms: props.interval_ms,\n            mode: props.mode,\n            auto_start: props.auto_start,\n            locale: env.locale.clone(),\n            messages: messages.clone(),\n            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n        })\n    }\n\n    // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a transition,\n    // so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.\n    fn initial_effects(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        _ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {\n        let mut effects = Vec::new();\n        if matches!(state, State::Running) {\n            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));\n        }\n        effects\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        match (state, event) {\n            // Idle start, paused resume, and paused start all enter `Running`\n            // and (re)emit the interval intent.\n            (State::Idle, Event::Start)\n            | (State::Paused, Event::Resume | Event::Start) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)))\n            }\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => match ctx.mode {\n                Mode::Countdown => {\n                    let new_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_sub(u64::from(ctx.interval_ms));\n                    if new_ms == 0 {\n                        Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                            .apply(|ctx| { ctx.current_ms = 0; })\n                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCompleted)))\n                    } else {\n                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                            ctx.current_ms = new_ms;\n                        }))\n                    }\n                }\n                Mode::Stopwatch => {\n                    let interval = u64::from(ctx.interval_ms);\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                        ctx.current_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_add(interval);\n                    }))\n                }\n            },\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Reset) => {\n                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {\n                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,\n                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                    .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current_ms = initial_ms; })\n                    .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {\n                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,\n                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                    .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current_ms = initial_ms; })\n                    .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(ms)) => {\n                let ms = *ms;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.current_ms = ms;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n```"

```
1	//! `spec compile-snippets` — parse Rust code blocks in spec markdown
2	//! through `syn::parse_file` and surface syntax errors.
3	//!
4	//! ## What this catches
5	//!
6	//! - Top-level Rust syntax bugs in spec snippets: missing braces,
7	//!   mistyped `pub fn`, unterminated string literals, unbalanced
8	//!   generics outside macro bodies, etc.
9	//! - Coverage across the full spec corpus —
10	//!   `spec/{components,foundation,leptos-components,dioxus-components,shared,testing}/`.
11	//!   The pre-existing `lint-code` linter only walked
12	//!   `spec/components/` and never invoked a Rust parser, so foundation
13	//!   and per-adapter specs had zero parse coverage.
14	//!
15	//! ## What this **does not** catch (important — read this)
16	//!
17	//! - **Macro-internal syntax.** `view! { … }` and `rsx! { … }` are
18	//!   opaque token trees to both `syn` and `rustc`; everything inside
19	//!   the outer braces is unchecked. The original Leptos `view!`
20	//!   regression (an inline `|errors: ArcRwSignal<Errors>|` closure
21	//!   where the macro parser misread `<Errors>` as an HTML tag) sits
22	//!   *inside* the macro and would not be caught here. The unit test
23	//!   suites at `crates/ars-{leptos,dioxus}/tests/` are the source of
24	//!   truth for macro-internal correctness.
25	//! - **Type / path resolution.** A snippet that names a type defined
26	//!   nowhere in the workspace still parses syntactically. Use
27	//!   `cargo check` against a fixture crate for that.
28	//! - **Borrow-checker / ownership errors.** Same as above —
29	//!   `syn::parse_file` is purely a parser.
30	//!
31	//! ## Opting out
32	//!
33	//! Code blocks that intentionally contain partial or pseudo-Rust
34	//! (signatures shown for documentation purposes, top-level expressions
35	//! meant to live inside an outer fn body, etc.) opt out by tagging the
36	//! fence info-string with `rust,no_check` (also recognised:
37	//! `rust,ignore`, `rust,no_run`, mirroring rustdoc). The extractor
38	//! honors those markers and skips the block.
39	//!
40	//! ## Performance
41	//!
42	//! Fast: no compiler invocation. Runs in milliseconds across all 2000+
43	//! Rust blocks in the spec, so it is suitable for inclusion in the
44	//! `cargo xci` pipeline (see `xtask::ci::Step::SpecCompileSnippets`).
45	
46	use std::{fs, path::Path};
47	
48	use crate::manifest::{Error, SpecRoot};
49	
50	/// CLI entry point.
51	///
52	/// Always returns `Ok(String)`. The text either begins with `ok:` (no
53	/// findings) or with `N finding(s):` followed by per-finding lines.
54	///
55	/// When `fix` is `true`, each failing block has its opening fence rewritten
56	/// from `` ```rust `` (or `` ```rs ``) to `` ```rust,no_check `` (or
57	/// `` ```rs,no_check ``) in place; the file is rewritten on disk and the
58	/// returned text reports how many fences were patched.
59	///
60	/// # Errors
61	///
62	/// Returns [`Error::Io`] when a target file cannot be read or written.
63	pub fn execute(root: &SpecRoot, fix: bool) -> Result<String, Error> {
64	    let mut files = Vec::new();
65	
66	    for sub in [
67	        "components",
68	        "foundation",
69	        "leptos-components",
70	        "dioxus-components",
71	        "shared",
72	        "testing",
73	    ] {
74	        let dir = root.path.join(sub);
75	
76	        collect_md_files(&dir, &mut files).map_err(Error::Io)?;
77	    }
78	
79	    files.sort();
80	
81	    let display_root = fs::canonicalize(&root.path).unwrap_or_else(|_| root.path.clone());
82	
83	    let mut findings = Vec::new();
84	    let mut blocks_checked = 0usize;
85	    let mut fences_patched = 0usize;
86	
87	    for path in &files {
88	        let source = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(Error::Io)?;
89	
90	        let display = path
91	            .strip_prefix(&display_root)
92	            .unwrap_or(path)
93	            .display()
94	            .to_string();
95	
96	        let blocks = extract_rust_blocks(&source);
97	
98	        let mut failing_fence_lines = Vec::new();
99	
100	        for block in &blocks {
101	            blocks_checked += 1;
102	
103	            if let Some(message) = check_block(block) {
104	                findings.push(Finding {
105	                    file: display.clone(),
106	                    line: block.start_line,
107	                    message,
108	                });
109	
110	                if fix {
111	                    // The fence line is one above the block's first content line.
112	                    failing_fence_lines.push(block.start_line.saturating_sub(1));
113	                }
114	            }
115	        }
116	
117	        if fix && !failing_fence_lines.is_empty() {
118	            let patched = rewrite_fences_to_no_check(&source, &failing_fence_lines);
119	
120	            if patched != source {
121	                let count = patched_fence_count(&source, &patched);
122	
123	                fences_patched += count;
124	
125	                fs::write(path, patched).map_err(Error::Io)?;
126	            }
127	        }
128	    }
129	
130	    let mut out = String::new();
131	
132	    if findings.is_empty() {
133	        out.push_str(&format!(
134	            "ok: {} block(s) parsed across {} spec file(s), no syntax findings\n",
135	            blocks_checked,
136	            files.len()
137	        ));
138	
139	        return Ok(out);
140	    }
141	
142	    if fix {
143	        // Re-run after patching so the report reflects the fixed state and
144	        // exit-code pipelines see a clean run on the next invocation.
145	        out.push_str(&format!(
146	            "fixed: patched {fences_patched} fence(s) across {} spec file(s) ({blocks_checked} \
147	             block(s) parsed). Re-run `cargo xtask spec compile-snippets` to confirm a clean \
148	             pass.\n",
149	            files.len()
150	        ));
151	
152	        return Ok(out);
153	    }
154	
155	    out.push_str(&format!(
156	        "{} finding(s) across {} spec file(s) ({} block(s) parsed):\n\n",
157	        findings.len(),
158	        files.len(),
159	        blocks_checked,
160	    ));
```

68:| start or resume click | timer not already running | `Start` or `Resume`           | timer effect begins after transition               | no timer starts during SSR                    |
69:| pause click           | running                   | `Pause`                       | clear active interval before leaving running state | display freezes at current value              |
70:| reset click           | any non-idle state        | `Reset`                       | clear active interval before resetting display     | countdown returns to original duration        |
71:| interval tick         | running                   | `Tick`                        | generated by platform timer effect                 | completion transition may cancel the interval |
77:| active tick interval | enter running state  | component instance | pause, reset, completion, prop change, or cleanup | clear interval | at most one active interval |
86:- adapter-local derived bookkeeping: active interval handle only.
97:| timer API unavailable                      | degrade gracefully | render the static display and controls without automatic ticking        |
98:| page visibility or throttling delays ticks | degrade gracefully | recompute from machine time source rather than trusting delayed cadence |
107:- Tick intervals are client-only.
108:- Hydration must preserve the same initial display before any auto-start logic runs.
112:- Keep only one interval active.
114:- Cancel intervals promptly when the timer leaves running state.

> AGENT

Implementation complete and audited. Here is the result for your review.

## Task #294 — Implement Timer agnostic core

### What landed

**New component** [crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs) — a stateful `Timer` machine:
- `Mode` (Countdown/Stopwatch), `State` (Idle/Running/Paused/Completed), `Event` (Start/Pause/Resume/Reset/Restart/Tick/SetTime), `Context`, `Props` (+ builder), `Messages` (6 i18n labels), and an 8-part `Part` enum / `Api` with `role="timer"`, `aria-live` region, `progressbar`, segment + formatted-time output, and state-dependent trigger labels/disabled states.
- Module export wired in [specialized/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs).

### Spec drift resolved (the notable deviation from the literal spec)

The spec's §1.5 machine impl predated current conventions and **would not compile**: it used string effect names, called nonexistent `set_interval`/`cancel_interval`, and did inline `use_platform_effects().announce()`. Per CLAUDE.md I resolved this in-PR — [spec/components/specialized/timer.md](spec/components/specialized/timer.md) now models the recurring tick + completion announcement as typed **marker effects** (`Effect::TimerInterval`, `Effect::AnnounceCompleted`) that adapters own, matching the `clipboard`/`toast` precedent. Auto-start emits the interval via `initial_effects`; the effect is cancelled whenever the timer leaves `Running`.

### Tests (TDD)
- **33 unit** + **17 snapshot** (committed `.snap`, inspected) covering every part across idle/running/paused/completed/hours branches.
- **1 spec-conformance** anatomy test, **1 proptest** (invariants: stopwatch never completes, countdown never exceeds target, connect never panics), **1 doctest** on the canonical `Machine` entry.

### Audit findings (all landed in this PR)
- **Phase 1 (drift):** none — impl matches the spec contract; spec synced to the Effect model. Builders/`Debug` are accepted impl-level conventions the spec omits (verified against clipboard).
- **Phase 2 (×2 clean rounds):** adapter specs already describe adapter-owned interval ownership (no stale API refs); no feature wiring, prelude, catalog, or new-dep changes needed.
- **Phase 3 (coverage):** added the doctest; fixed broken intra-doc links my docs introduced. Production code coverage 99% (only uncovered region is the no-op test callback — an expected non-gap).

### Verification
| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| `clippy -p ars-components --all-targets -D warnings` (default + i18n) | clean |
| lib tests | 4628 passed |
| spec_conformance | 115 passed |
| doctest (timer) | 1 passed |
| proptest (timer, 256+512 cases) | passed |
| `cargo doc` (timer module) | zero warnings |
| `xtask spec validate` + `compile-snippets` | passed |

No commit/push yet (awaiting your approval). Once you approve, I'll run `cargo xci-fast`, open a PR to `main` with `Closes #294` + the `snapshot-reviewed` label, then run the Codex-review loop.

> AGENT

The user's `Duration` standard has clear precedent: `search_input::Props.debounce: Option<Duration>` and `scroll_area::Props.hide_delay: Duration` both use `core::time::Duration` (no_std-compatible) with no `_ms` suffix. (Some older components — clipboard, toast, navigation_menu — still use `_ms` integers, but those are out of scope for this task.) I'll convert Timer to `Duration`, matching that convention, and sync the spec.

Let me save this as durable feedback first, then make the change.

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01XcVW5TNXG24jL1byzs5RsS
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/.claude/projects/-Users-ericson-Workspace-Rust-ars-ui/memory/feedback_duration_for_time_fields.md",
  "content": "---\nname: feedback_duration_for_time_fields\ndescription: Use core::time::Duration for time-related fields, never u32/u64 milliseconds\nmetadata:\n  type: feedback\n---\n\nTime-related fields (durations, intervals, delays, timeouts, elapsed/remaining time) in component `Props`/`Context`/`Event`/`Api` must use `core::time::Duration`, never raw `u32`/`u64` milliseconds. Drop the `_ms` suffix when the type is `Duration`.\n\n**Why:** Duration is type-safe (no unit confusion), self-documenting, and is the established workspace standard in the newer components.\n\n**How to apply:** Follow the precedent in `crates/ars-components/src/input/search_input/mod.rs` (`debounce: Option<Duration>`) and `crates/ars-components/src/layout/scroll_area/mod.rs` (`hide_delay: Duration`). `core::time::Duration` is no_std-compatible. Use `Duration::from_millis/from_secs`, `as_millis`/`as_secs_f64`, `is_zero`, `saturating_add`/`saturating_sub`, `Duration::ZERO`.\n\nHoldouts still using `_ms` integers (clipboard `feedback_duration_ms`, toast duration, navigation_menu `delay_ms`/`skip_delay_ms`, and the low-level `PlatformEffects::set_timeout(delay_ms: u32)`) predate this rule; convert them when touched, not preemptively. Related: [[feedback_use_key_not_string]].\n"
}
```
