{
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs",
  "content": "//! Timer component state machine and connect API.\n//!\n//! The `Timer` implements countdown or stopwatch functionality with start,\n//! pause, resume, reset, and restart controls. It ticks at a configurable\n//! interval and transitions to a [`State::Completed`] state when a countdown\n//! reaches zero.\n//!\n//! The agnostic core only emits typed `Effect` intents and exposes the `Api`\n//! connect surface; framework adapters translate\n//! `Effect::TimerInterval` into a real recurring interval (e.g. `setInterval`)\n//! that dispatches `Event::Tick`, and translate `Effect::AnnounceCompleted`\n//! into a live-region announcement.\n\nuse alloc::{format, string::String, vec::Vec};\nuse core::{\n    fmt::{self, Debug},\n    time::Duration,\n};\n\nuse ars_core::{\n    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, CssProperty,\n    Env, HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan,\n};\n\n/// Timer mode.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]\npub enum Mode {\n    /// Counts down from a target duration to zero.\n    #[default]\n    Countdown,\n\n    /// Counts up from zero indefinitely.\n    Stopwatch,\n}\n\n/// The state of the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum State {\n    /// Timer is not running, at its initial value.\n    Idle,\n\n    /// Timer is actively ticking.\n    Running,\n\n    /// Timer is paused, preserving its current value.\n    Paused,\n\n    /// Countdown reached zero (only for [`Mode::Countdown`]).\n    Completed,\n}\n\n/// Events for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum Event {\n    /// Start or resume the timer.\n    Start,\n\n    /// Pause the timer.\n    Pause,\n\n    /// Resume from the paused state.\n    Resume,\n\n    /// Reset to the initial value.\n    Reset,\n\n    /// Reset to the initial value and immediately start.\n    Restart,\n\n    /// A single tick interval elapsed (dispatched by the adapter's interval).\n    Tick,\n\n    /// Set the remaining/elapsed time directly.\n    SetTime(Duration),\n}\n\n/// Context for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// Current time value. For countdown this is the remaining time; for\n    /// stopwatch this is the elapsed time.\n    pub current: Duration,\n\n    /// The target duration (for countdown).\n    pub target: Duration,\n\n    /// Tick interval.\n    pub interval: Duration,\n\n    /// Timer mode.\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Whether auto-start is enabled.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n\n    /// Active locale inherited from provider context.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n\n    /// Component instance IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n}\n\n/// Props for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// Component instance ID.\n    pub id: String,\n\n    /// Target duration (for countdown mode).\n    pub target: Duration,\n\n    /// Tick interval.\n    pub interval: Duration,\n\n    /// Timer mode (countdown or stopwatch).\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Auto-start when mounted.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            target: Duration::from_secs(60),\n            interval: Duration::from_secs(1),\n            mode: Mode::Countdown,\n            auto_start: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Props {\n    /// Returns a fresh [`Props`] with every field at its [`Default`] value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`id`](Self::id).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.id = id.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`target`](Self::target).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn target(mut self, target: Duration) -> Self {\n        self.target = target;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`interval`](Self::interval).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn interval(mut self, interval: Duration) -> Self {\n        self.interval = interval;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`mode`](Self::mode).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn mode(mut self, mode: Mode) -> Self {\n        self.mode = mode;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`auto_start`](Self::auto_start).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn auto_start(mut self, auto_start: bool) -> Self {\n        self.auto_start = auto_start;\n        self\n    }\n}\n\n/// Messages for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Messages {\n    /// Start button label. Default: `\"Start timer\"`.\n    pub start_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Pause button label. Default: `\"Pause timer\"`.\n    pub pause_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Reset button label. Default: `\"Reset timer\"`.\n    pub reset_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Resume button label. Default: `\"Resume timer\"`.\n    pub resume_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Progress bar label. Default: `\"Timer progress\"`.\n    pub progress_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n\n    /// Completion announcement. Default: `\"Timer completed\"`.\n    pub completed_announcement: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Messages {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            start_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Start timer\"),\n            pause_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Pause timer\"),\n            reset_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Reset timer\"),\n            resume_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Resume timer\"),\n            progress_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Timer progress\"),\n            completed_announcement: MessageFn::static_str(\"Timer completed\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n/// Typed effect intents emitted by the timer machine.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    /// Adapter starts a recurring interval of [`Context::interval`] that\n    /// dispatches [`Event::Tick`] on each elapse. Emitted on initial mount when\n    /// the timer auto-starts (via\n    /// [`Machine::initial_effects`](ars_core::Machine::initial_effects)) and on\n    /// every transition into [`State::Running`]. Cancelled whenever the timer\n    /// leaves [`State::Running`] (pause, completion, reset, or restart); the\n    /// adapter no-ops the cancellation when no interval is active.\n    TimerInterval,\n\n    /// Adapter announces the [`Messages::completed_announcement`] message into a\n    /// polite `aria-live` region. Emitted on the countdown transition into\n    /// [`State::Completed`].\n    AnnounceCompleted,\n}\n\n/// The machine for the `Timer` component.\n///\n/// # Examples\n///\n/// Drive a two-second countdown to completion. Each [`Event::Tick`] is\n/// dispatched by the adapter-owned interval started by\n/// [`Effect::TimerInterval`]; here we send them directly:\n///\n/// ```\n/// use core::time::Duration;\n///\n/// use ars_components::specialized::timer::{Event, Machine, Messages, Props, State};\n/// use ars_core::{Env, Service};\n///\n/// let mut timer = Service::<Machine>::new(\n///     Props::new()\n///         .id(\"egg\")\n///         .target(Duration::from_secs(2))\n///         .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n///     &Env::default(),\n///     &Messages::default(),\n/// );\n///\n/// drop(timer.send(Event::Start));\n/// assert_eq!(timer.state(), &State::Running);\n///\n/// drop(timer.send(Event::Tick)); // 2s -> 1s remaining\n/// assert_eq!(timer.context().current, Duration::from_secs(1));\n///\n/// drop(timer.send(Event::Tick)); // 1s -> 0s: the countdown completes\n/// assert_eq!(timer.state(), &State::Completed);\n/// assert_eq!(timer.connect(&|_| {}).formatted_time(), \"00:00\");\n/// ```\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        let current = initial_duration(props.mode, props.target);\n\n        let state = if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n        };\n\n        (\n            state,\n            Context {\n                current,\n                target: props.target,\n                interval: props.interval,\n                mode: props.mode,\n                auto_start: props.auto_start,\n                locale: env.locale.clone(),\n                messages: messages.clone(),\n                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            },\n        )\n    }\n\n    fn initial_effects(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        _ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {\n        let mut effects = Vec::new();\n\n        // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a\n        // transition, so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.\n        if matches!(state, State::Running) {\n            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));\n        }\n\n        effects\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        match (state, event) {\n            // Idle start, paused resume, and paused start all enter `Running`\n            // and (re)emit the interval intent.\n            (State::Idle, Event::Start) | (State::Paused, Event::Resume | Event::Start) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_effect(interval_effect()))\n            }\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => match ctx.mode {\n                Mode::Countdown => {\n                    let new = ctx.current.saturating_sub(ctx.interval);\n                    if new.is_zero() {\n                        Some(\n                            TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                                    ctx.current = Duration::ZERO;\n                                })\n                                .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                                .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCompleted)),\n                        )\n                    } else {\n                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current = new;\n                        }))\n                    }\n                }\n                Mode::Stopwatch => {\n                    let interval = ctx.interval;\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.current = ctx.current.saturating_add(interval);\n                    }))\n                }\n            },\n\n            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Reset) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current = initial;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),\n                )\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current = initial;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)),\n                )\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(duration)) => {\n                let duration = *duration;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.current = duration;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// DOM parts of the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"timer\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// Root wrapper element (`role=\"timer\"`, `aria-live` region).\n    Root,\n\n    /// Label describing the timer.\n    Label,\n\n    /// Formatted time-string display.\n    Display,\n\n    /// Progress indicator (`role=\"progressbar\"`).\n    Progress,\n\n    /// Start/resume button.\n    StartTrigger,\n\n    /// Pause button.\n    PauseTrigger,\n\n    /// Reset button.\n    ResetTrigger,\n\n    /// Decorative colon between time segments.\n    Separator,\n}\n\n/// API for the `Timer` component.\npub struct Api<'a> {\n    state: &'a State,\n    ctx: &'a Context,\n    props: &'a Props,\n    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Api<'_> {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"timer::Api\")\n            .field(\"state\", self.state)\n            .field(\"ctx\", self.ctx)\n            .field(\"props\", self.props)\n            .finish_non_exhaustive()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Api<'_> {\n    /// Whether the timer is actively ticking.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_running(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Running)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the timer is paused.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_paused(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Paused)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the countdown has completed.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_completed(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Completed)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the timer is idle at its initial value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_idle(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Idle)\n    }\n\n    /// The current time value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn current(&self) -> Duration {\n        self.ctx.current\n    }\n\n    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, and milliseconds.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {\n        let total_ms = self.ctx.current.as_millis() as u64;\n        let hours = total_ms / 3_600_000;\n        let minutes = (total_ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;\n        let seconds = (total_ms % 60_000) / 1_000;\n        let millis = total_ms % 1_000;\n        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)\n    }\n\n    /// Progress as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.\n    ///\n    /// For countdown this is the fraction of [`Context::target`] already\n    /// elapsed; for stopwatch this is the elapsed time as a fraction of\n    /// [`Context::target`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (`HH:MM:SS` when hours are present, else `MM:SS`).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _millis) = self.display_time();\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\"{hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        }\n    }\n\n    const fn state_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self.state {\n            State::Idle => \"idle\",\n            State::Running => \"running\",\n            State::Paused => \"paused\",\n            State::Completed => \"completed\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Root element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"timer\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_str())\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), \"polite\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), \"true\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.formatted_time());\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Label element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"));\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Display element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn display_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Display.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"display\"))\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Progress indicator attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Progress.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"progressbar\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow),\n                format!(\"{:.0}\", self.progress() * 100.0),\n            )\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMin), \"0\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueMax), \"100\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.progress_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            )\n            .set_style(\n                CssProperty::Custom(\"ars-timer-progress\"),\n                format!(\"{:.2}\", self.progress()),\n            );\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Start/resume trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn start_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::StartTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                if self.is_paused() {\n                    (self.ctx.messages.resume_label)(&self.ctx.locale)\n                } else {\n                    (self.ctx.messages.start_label)(&self.ctx.locale)\n                },\n            );\n\n        if self.is_running() || self.is_completed() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Pause trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pause_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::PauseTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.pause_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if !self.is_running() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Reset trigger attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn reset_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ResetTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.reset_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if self.is_idle() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Separator element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn separator_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Separator.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Hidden), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches start/resume intent from the start trigger.\n    pub fn on_start_trigger_click(&self) {\n        if self.is_paused() {\n            (self.send)(Event::Resume);\n        } else {\n            (self.send)(Event::Start);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches pause intent from the pause trigger.\n    pub fn on_pause_trigger_click(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Pause);\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches reset intent from the reset trigger.\n    pub fn on_reset_trigger_click(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Reset);\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches restart intent (reset and immediately start).\n    pub fn on_restart(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Restart);\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),\n            Part::Display => self.display_attrs(),\n            Part::Progress => self.progress_attrs(),\n            Part::StartTrigger => self.start_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::PauseTrigger => self.pause_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::ResetTrigger => self.reset_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::Separator => self.separator_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// The initial `current` value for a given mode and target.\nconst fn initial_duration(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Duration {\n    match mode {\n        Mode::Countdown => target,\n        Mode::Stopwatch => Duration::ZERO,\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds the marker effect that starts the adapter-owned tick interval.\nfn interval_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use alloc::{boxed::Box, sync::Arc, vec, vec::Vec};\n    use std::sync::Mutex;\n\n    use ars_core::{Machine as _, Service};\n    use insta::assert_snapshot;\n\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn test_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().id(\"timer\")\n    }\n\n    fn fresh_service(props: Props) -> Service<Machine> {\n        Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default())\n    }\n\n    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n        format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n    }\n\n    fn effect_names(result: &ars_core::SendResult<Machine>) -> Vec<Effect> {\n        result\n            .pending_effects\n            .iter()\n            .map(|effect| effect.name)\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    /// Leaks a fully-formed [`Api`] in an arbitrary state for attribute and\n    /// accessor assertions, mirroring the clipboard test helper.\n    fn api_for(state: State, current: Duration, mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Api<'static> {\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(Props::new().id(\"timer\").mode(mode).target(target)));\n        let messages = Messages::default();\n        let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);\n        ctx.current = current;\n\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));\n\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Props ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_props_default_matches_spec() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"\");\n        assert_eq!(props.target, Duration::from_secs(60));\n        assert_eq!(props.interval, Duration::from_secs(1));\n        assert_eq!(props.mode, Mode::Countdown);\n        assert!(!props.auto_start);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_props_builder_sets_expected_fields() {\n        let props = Props::new()\n            .id(\"timer\")\n            .target(Duration::from_secs(5))\n            .interval(Duration::from_millis(250))\n            .mode(Mode::Stopwatch)\n            .auto_start(true);\n\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"timer\");\n        assert_eq!(props.target, Duration::from_secs(5));\n        assert_eq!(props.interval, Duration::from_millis(250));\n        assert_eq!(props.mode, Mode::Stopwatch);\n        assert!(props.auto_start);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_mode_default_is_countdown() {\n        assert_eq!(Mode::default(), Mode::Countdown);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── init ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_init_starts_idle_at_target() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(30)));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(30));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().target, Duration::from_secs(30));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().mode, Mode::Countdown);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), \"timer\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_stopwatch_init_starts_idle_at_zero() {\n        let service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .mode(Mode::Stopwatch)\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(30)),\n        );\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_auto_start_boots_running_and_emits_interval_effect() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().auto_start(true));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(\n            service\n                .take_initial_effects()\n                .iter()\n                .map(|effect| effect.name)\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),\n            vec![Effect::TimerInterval]\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_without_auto_start_emits_no_initial_effects() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.take_initial_effects().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_full_lifecycle_idle_running_paused_running_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(2))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n\n        let started = service.send(Event::Start);\n        assert!(started.state_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&started), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let tick = service.send(Event::Tick);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(1));\n        assert!(tick.pending_effects.is_empty());\n\n        let paused = service.send(Event::Pause);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Paused);\n        assert_eq!(paused.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let resumed = service.send(Event::Resume);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&resumed), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        let completed = service.send(Event::Tick);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n        assert_eq!(completed.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&completed), vec![Effect::AnnounceCompleted]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_tick_decrements_by_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(10))\n                .interval(Duration::from_millis(2_500)),\n        );\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_millis(7_500));\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(5));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_countdown_tick_at_or_below_interval_completes() {\n        // current (500ms) < interval (1s) saturates to zero and completes.\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_millis(500))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::Tick);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::AnnounceCompleted]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_stopwatch_tick_increments_from_zero() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .mode(Mode::Stopwatch)\n service.send(Event::Reset);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(10));\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_reset_stopwatch_returns_to_zero() {\n        let mut service = fn timer_set_time_updates_current_without_changing_state() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(10)));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n Duration::from_millis(3_333));\n        assert!(result.context_changed);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_ignores_unhandled_events() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n while idle is a no-op.\n        let result = service.send(Event::Pause);\n        assert!(!result.state_changed);\n\n        // Start from completed is rejected.\n        let mut service = assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_api_state_predicates_match_state() {\n        let #[test]\n    fn timer_api_progress_countdown_and_stopwatch() {\n        let one_sec = Duration::from_secs(1);\n        let half_sec = Duration::from_millis(500);\n\n        // Countdown: progress is = Arc::clone(&events);\n            let sink = move |event| captured.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n            paused.connect(&sink).on_start_trigger_click();\n        }\n\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        {\n            let captured = Arc::clone(&events);\n            let sink = move |event| captured.lock().unwrap().push(event);\n            let api = 729	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
730	// Timer
731	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
732	
733	mod timer_proptests {
734	    use ars_components::specialized::timer::{Event, Machine, Mode, Props, State};
735	    use ars_core::{Env, Service};
736	    use proptest::prelude::*;
737	
738	    fn arb_mode() -> impl Strategy<Value = Mode> {
739	        prop_oneof![Just(Mode::Countdown), Just(Mode::Stopwatch)]
740	    }
741	
742	    prop_compose! {
743	        fn arb_props()(
744	            mode in arb_mode(),
745	            target_ms in 1_000u64..=600_000,
746	            interval_ms in 1u32..=60_000,
747	            auto_start in any::<bool>(),
748	        ) -> Props {
749	            Props::new()
750	                .id("timer")
751	                .mode(mode)
752	                .target_ms(target_ms)
753	                .interval_ms(interval_ms)
754	                .auto_start(auto_start)
755	        }
756	    }
757	
758	    /// Events parameterised by `target` so `SetTime` stays within the target
759	    /// range; this keeps the "countdown never exceeds target" invariant true
760	    /// while still exercising the `SetTime` branch.
761	    fn arb_event(target: u64) -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {
762	        prop_oneof![
763	            Just(Event::Start),
764	            Just(Event::Pause),
765	            Just(Event::Resume),
766	            Just(Event::Reset),
767	            Just(Event::Restart),
768	            Just(Event::Tick),
769	            (0u64..=target).prop_map(Event::SetTime),
770	        ]
771	    }
772	
773	    fn arb_scenario() -> impl Strategy<Value = (Props, Vec<Event>)> {
774	        arb_props().prop_flat_map(|props| {
775	            let events = prop::collection::vec(arb_event(props.target_ms), 0..128);
776	
777	            (Just(props), events)
778	        })
779	    }
780	
781	    proptest! {
782	        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]
783	
784	        #[test]
785	        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
786	        fn timer_event_sequences_preserve_invariants((props, events) in arb_scenario()) {
787	            let mode = props.mode;
788	            let target_ms = props.target_ms;
789	
790	            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());
791	
792	            drop(svc.take_initial_effects());
793	
794	            for ev in events {
795	                drop(svc.send(ev));
796	
797	                let state = *svc.state();
798	                let current = svc.context().current_ms;
799	
800	                // Stopwatch mode can never reach the completed state; only a
801	                // countdown Tick reaching zero produces `Completed`. (`SetTime`
802	                // is unconditional and may set a non-zero value while completed,
803	                // so completion-at-zero is asserted in the unit lifecycle test.)
804	                if mode == Mode::Stopwatch {
805	                    prop_assert_ne!(state, State::Completed);
806	                }
807	
808	                // Countdown never exceeds its target (SetTime is bounded to it).
809	                if mode == Mode::Countdown {
810	                    prop_assert!(current <= target_ms);
811	                }
812	
813	                // `connect()` and its output methods never panic and always
814	                // produce finite progress and a non-empty formatted time.
815	                let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});
816	
817	                prop_assert!(api.progress().is_finite());
818	                prop_assert!(!api.formatted_time().is_empty());
819	
820	                drop(api.root_attrs());
821	                drop(api.progress_attrs());
822	                drop(api.start_trigger_attrs());
823	                drop(api.pause_trigger_attrs());
824	                drop(api.reset_trigger_attrs());
825	            }
826	        }
827	    }
828	}
829

{\n        fn arb_props()(\n            mode in arb_mode(),\n            target_ms in 1_000u64..=600_000,\n            interval_ms in 1u32..=60_000,\n            auto_start in any::<bool>(),\n        ) -> Props {\n            Props::new()\n                .id(\"timer\")\n                .mode(mode)\n                .target_ms(target_ms)\n                .interval_ms(interval_ms)\n                .auto_start(auto_start)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Events parameterised by `target` so `SetTime` stays within the target\n    /// range; this keeps the \"countdown never exceeds target\" invariant true\n    /// while still exercising the `SetTime` branch.\n    fn arb_event(target: u64) -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            Just(Event::Start),\n            Just(Event::Pause),\n            Just(Event::Resume),\n            Just(Event::Reset),\n            Just(Event::Restart),\n            Just(Event::Tick),\n            (0u64..=target).prop_map(Event::SetTime),\n        ]\n    }\n\n    fn arb_scenario() -> impl Strategy<Value = (Props, Vec<Event>)> {\n        arb_props().prop_flat_map(|props| {\n            let events = prop::collection::vec(arb_event(props.target_ms), 0..128);\n\n            (Just(props), events)\n        })\n    }\n\n    proptest! {\n        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]\n\n        #[test]\n        #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n        fn timer_event_sequences_preserve_invariants((props, events) in arb_scenario()) {\n            let mode = props.mode;\n            let target_ms = props.target_ms;\n\n            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n\n            drop(svc.take_initial_effects());\n\n            for ev in events {\n                drop(svc.send(ev));\n\n                let state = *svc.state();\n                let current = svc.context().current_ms;\n\n                // Stopwatch mode can never reach the completed state; only a\n                // countdown Tick reaching zero produces `Completed`. (`SetTime`\n                // is unconditional and may set a non-zero value while completed,\n                // so completion-at-zero is asserted in the unit lifecycle test.)\n                if mode == Mode::Stopwatch {\n                    prop_assert_ne!(state, State::Completed);\n                }\n\n                // Countdown never exceeds its target (SetTime is bounded to it).\n                if mode == Mode::Countdown {\n                    prop_assert!(current <= target_ms);\n                }",
  "new_string": "mod timer_proptests {\n    use core::time::Duration;\n\n    use ars_components::specialized::timer::{Event, Machine, Mode, Props, State};\n    use ars_core::{Env, Service};\n    use proptest::prelude::*;\n\n    fn arb_mode() -> impl Strategy<Value = Mode> {\n        prop_oneof![Just(Mode::Countdown), Just(Mode::Stopwatch)]\n    }\n\n    prop_compose! {\n        fn arb_props()(\n            mode in arb_mode(),\n            target_ms in 1_000u64..=600_000,\n            interval_ms in 1u64..=60_000,\n            auto_start in any::<bool>(),\n        ) -> Props {\n            Props::new()\n                .id(\"timer\")\n                .mode(mode)\n                .target(Duration::from_millis(target_ms))\n                .interval(Duration::from_millis(interval_ms))\n                .auto_start(auto_start)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Events parameterised by `target` so `SetTime` stays within the target\n    /// range; this keeps the \"countdown never exceeds target\" invariant true\n    /// while still exercising the `SetTime` branch.\n    fn arb_event(target: Duration) -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {\n        let target_ms = u64::try_from(target.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);\n        prop_oneof![\n            Just(Event::Start),\n            Just(Event::Pause),\n            Just(Event::Resume),\n            Just(Event::Reset),\n            Just(Event::Restart),\n            Just(Event::Tick),\n            (0u64..=target_ms).prop_map(|ms| Event::SetTime(Duration::from_millis(ms))),\n        ]\n    }\n\n    fn arb_scenario() -> impl Strategy<Value = (Props, Vec<Event>)> {\n        arb_props().prop_flat_map(|props| {\n            let events = prop::collection::vec(arb_event(props.target), 0..128);\n\n            (Just(props), events)\n        })\n    }\n\n    proptest! {\n        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]\n\n        #[test]\n        #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n        fn timer_event_sequences_preserve_invariants((props, events) in arb_scenario()) {\n            let mode = props.mode;\n            let target = props.target;\n\n            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Default::default());\n\n            drop(svc.take_initial_effects());\n\n            for ev in events {\n                drop(svc.send(ev));\n\n                let state = *svc.state();\n                let current = svc.context().current;\n\n                // Stopwatch mode can never reach the completed state; only a\n                // countdown Tick reaching zero produces `Completed`. (`SetTime`\n                // is unconditional and may set a non-zero value while completed,\n                // so completion-at-zero is asserted in the unit lifecycle test.)\n                if mode == Mode::Stopwatch {\n                    prop_assert_ne!(state, State::Completed);\n                }\n\n                // Countdown never exceeds its target (SetTime is bounded to it).\n                if mode == Mode::Countdown {\n                    prop_assert!(current <= target);\n                }"
}

164	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
165	
166	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
167	        let current_ms = match props.mode {
168	            Mode::Countdown => props.target_ms,
169	            Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
170	        };
171	
172	        let state = if props.auto_start {
173	            State::Running
174	        } else {
175	            State::Idle
176	        };
177	
178	        (state, Context {
179	            current_ms,
180	            target_ms: props.target_ms,
181	            interval_ms: props.interval_ms,
182	            mode: props.mode,
183	            auto_start: props.auto_start,
184	            locale: env.locale.clone(),
185	            messages: messages.clone(),
186	            ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
187	        })
188	    }
189	
190	    // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a transition,
191	    // so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.
192	    fn initial_effects(
193	        state: &Self::State,
194	        _ctx: &Self::Context,
195	        _props: &Self::Props,
196	    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {
197	        let mut effects = Vec::new();
198	        if matches!(state, State::Running) {
199	            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));
200	        }
201	        effects
202	    }
203	
204	    fn transition(
205	        state: &Self::State,
206	        event: &Self::Event,
207	        ctx: &Self::Context,
208	        _props: &Self::Props,
209	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
210	        match (state, event) {
211	            // Idle start, paused resume, and paused start all enter `Running`
212	            // and (re)emit the interval intent.
213	            (State::Idle, Event::Start)
214	            | (State::Paused, Event::Resume | Event::Start) => {
215	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)
216	                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)))
217	            }
218	
219	            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => match ctx.mode {
220	                Mode::Countdown => {
221	                    let new_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_sub(u64::from(ctx.interval_ms));
222	                    if new_ms == 0 {
223	                        Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)
224	                            .apply(|ctx| { ctx.current_ms = 0; })
225	                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
226	                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCompleted)))
227	                    } else {
228	                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
229	                            ctx.current_ms = new_ms;
230	                        }))
231	                    }
232	                }
233	                Mode::Stopwatch => {
234	                    let interval = u64::from(ctx.interval_ms);
235	                    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
236	                        ctx.current_ms = ctx.current_ms.saturating_add(interval);
237	                    }))
238	                }
239	            },
240	
241	            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {
242	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))
243	            }
244	
245	            (_, Event::Reset) => {
246	                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {
247	                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,
248	                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
249	                };
250	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
251	                    .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current_ms = initial_ms; })
252	                    .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))
253	            }
254	
255	            (_, Event::Restart) => {
256	                let initial_ms = match ctx.mode {
257	                    Mode::Countdown => ctx.target_ms,
258	                    Mode::Stopwatch => 0,
259	                };
260	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)
261	                    .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current_ms = initial_ms; })
262	                    .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
263	                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)))
264	            }
265	
266	            (_, Event::SetTime(ms)) => {

{

  "old_string": "    pub fn current_ms(&self) -> u64 { self.ctx.current_ms }\n\n    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, milliseconds.\n    pub fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {\n        let ms = self.ctx.current_ms;\n        let hours = ms / 3_600_000;\n        let minutes = (ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;\n        let seconds = (ms % 60_000) / 1_000;\n        let millis = ms % 1_000;\n        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)\n    }\n\n    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0] (countdown only).\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target_ms == 0 { return 0.0; }\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - (self.ctx.current_ms as f64 / self.ctx.target_ms as f64),\n            Mode::Stopwatch => self.ctx.current_ms as f64 / self.ctx.target_ms as f64,\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    pub fn current(&self) -> Duration { self.ctx.current }\n\n    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, milliseconds.\n    pub fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {\n        let ms = self.ctx.current.as_millis() as u64;\n        let hours = ms / 3_600_000;\n        let minutes = (ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;\n        let seconds = (ms % 60_000) / 1_000;\n        let millis = ms % 1_000;\n        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)\n    }\n\n    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0] (countdown only).\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() { return 0.0; }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        }\n    }"
}

> AGENT

Done. All time-related fields now use `core::time::Duration`.

## `Duration` migration applied

Following the workspace standard (precedent: `search_input::Props.debounce: Option<Duration>`, `scroll_area::Props.hide_delay: Duration`), I converted every Timer time field off raw milliseconds:

| Before | After |
|---|---|
| `Props.target_ms: u64`, `interval_ms: u32` | `Props.target: Duration`, `interval: Duration` |
| `Context.current_ms/target_ms/interval_ms` | `Context.current/target/interval: Duration` |
| `Event::SetTime(u64)` | `Event::SetTime(Duration)` |
| `Api::current_ms() -> u64` | `Api::current() -> Duration` |
| builders `.target_ms(u64)/.interval_ms(u32)` | `.target(Duration)/.interval(Duration)` |

Internals now use `Duration::saturating_sub`/`saturating_add`/`is_zero`/`ZERO`/`from_secs`/`as_millis`/`as_secs_f64`. `display_time()` still returns a `(u64,u64,u64,u64)` segment tuple — those are decomposed display counts (hours/minutes/seconds/millis for rendering), not stored duration fields.

Landed across all four surfaces: [timer/mod.rs](crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs) (impl + 33 tests + doctest), the [timer proptest](crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs), and the [spec](spec/components/specialized/timer.md) (§1.2/1.3/1.4/1.6/1.7 + §5.1 parity table).

I also saved this as a durable convention in memory ([feedback_duration_for_time_fields](/Users/ericson/.claude/projects/-Users-ericson-Workspace-Rust-ars-ui/memory/feedback_duration_for_time_fields.md)) so future components default to `Duration`. Note: older components (`clipboard.feedback_duration_ms`, `toast` duration, `navigation_menu.delay_ms/skip_delay_ms`, and the low-level `PlatformEffects::set_timeout(delay_ms: u32)`) still use `_ms` integers — I left those untouched as out-of-scope for #294; flag me if you'd like a separate sweep.

### Verification (re-run after migration)
| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| `clippy --all-targets -D warnings` (default + i18n) | clean |
| lib tests | 4628 passed |
| spec_conformance | 115 passed |
| doctest (timer) | 1 passed |
| proptest (timer, 512 cases) | passed |
| snapshots | unchanged (identical AttrMap output) |
| `cargo doc` (timer module) | zero warnings |
| `xtask spec validate` + `compile-snippets` | passed |

Still no commit/push (awaiting approval). On your go: `cargo xci-fast`, PR to `main` with `Closes #294` + `snapshot-reviewed` label, then the Codex-review loop.

Plus `display_time`, `formatted_time`, `progress`.\n\n## Design notes (spec drift resolved in-PR)\n\nThe spec's original §1.5 machine impl would not compile against current conventions. Resolved per CLAUDE.md (spec/code mismatch in the same PR):\n\n- Recurring tick + completion announcement modelled as typed **marker effects** (`Effect::TimerInterval`, `Effect::AnnounceCompleted`) owned by adapters — matching the `clipboard`/`toast` precedent (no nonexistent `set_interval`, no inline `use_platform_effects`). Auto-start emits the interval via `initial_effects`; the interval is cancelled whenever the timer leaves `Running`.\n- All time-related fields use **`core::time::Duration`** (no raw `u32`/`u64` milliseconds), matching the `search_input`/`scroll_area` convention.\n\nSpec §1.2/1.3/1.4/1.6/1.7 + §5.1 synced to the Effect-enum + `Duration` model.\n\n## Tests\n\n- 33 unit + 17 snapshot (committed `.snap`, inspected) covering every part across idle/running/paused/completed/hours branches\n- 1 spec-conformance anatomy test, 1 proptest invariant block, 1 doctest\n- `post-implementation-audit` run (spec-drift, 2× \"anything missing\", coverage); doctest added, intra-doc links fixed\n\n## Verification\n\n`cargo xci-fast`: all 10 gates pass (fmt, clippy, unit, integration, adapter, spec-compile-snippets, error-variant-coverage, mutual-exclusion, snapshot-count, coverage-native). `ars-components` coverage 98.4% line / 90.2% branch.\n\nCloses #294\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nBODY\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

        .expect("hard-coded fallback date is valid");

        calendar.months_in_year(&date)
    }

=== format_segment_digits call site + separator handling ===
    backend: &dyn IntlBackend,
    locale: &Locale,
    kind: DateSegmentKind,
    value: i32,
    force_leading_zeros: bool,
) -> String {
    if kind == DateSegmentKind::DayPeriod {
        return backend.day_period_label(value == 1, locale);
    }

    let min_digits = if force_leading_zeros { 2 } else { 1 };

    u32::try_from(value).map_or_else(
        |_| value.to_string(),
        |value| {
            backend.format_segment_digits(
                value,
                NonZeroU8::new(min_digits).expect("segment width is non-zero"),
                locale,
            )
        },
    )
}

fn clamp_time(time: Time, min_value: Option<&Time>, max_value: Option<&Time>) -> Time {
    if let Some(min) = min_value
        && time < *min
    {
        return *min;
    }

    if let Some(max) = max_value
        && time > *max
    {
        return *max;
    }

    }
}

> AGENT

`Env::default()` uses `StubIntlBackend`, and foundation architecture doc explicitly states: *"Non-date-time components ignore `intl_backend` (it defaults to `StubIntlBackend`)."* Timer is a `specialized` (non-date-time) component — yet its own spec §4.1 requires locale-aware digits, and `time_field` is the precedent for threading the backend through.

Findings #1 (clamp progress) and #2 (`type="button"`) are unambiguous — I'll fix those regardless. Finding #3 sits at a genuine tension between two spec sources (foundation architecture vs the component's §4.1), so I want your call on direction before I implement.

        meta_key: false,
        repeat: false,

  "old_string": "/// Context for the `Timer` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// Current time value. For countdown this is the remaining time; for\n    /// stopwatch this is the elapsed time.\n    pub current: Duration,\n\n    /// The target duration (for countdown).\n    pub target: Duration,\n\n    /// Tick interval.\n    pub interval: Duration,\n\n    /// Timer mode.\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Whether auto-start is enabled.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n\n    /// Active locale inherited from provider context.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n\n    /// Component instance IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Context for the `Timer` component.\n///\n/// Cloneable but not `Debug`/`PartialEq`-derivable because [`Context::intl_backend`]\n/// is a trait object; both impls are provided manually and exclude the backend\n/// (it is an injected service, not observable state), mirroring\n/// [`time_field`](crate::date_time::time_field).\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// Current time value. For countdown this is the remaining time; for\n    /// stopwatch this is the elapsed time.\n    pub current: Duration,\n\n    /// The target duration (for countdown).\n    pub target: Duration,\n\n    /// Tick interval.\n    pub interval: Duration,\n\n    /// Timer mode.\n    pub mode: Mode,\n\n    /// Whether auto-start is enabled.\n    pub auto_start: bool,\n\n    /// Active locale inherited from provider context.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n\n    /// Component instance IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n\n    /// Backend used for locale-aware digit formatting of the displayed time.\n    pub intl_backend: Arc<dyn IntlBackend>,\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Context {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"timer::Context\")\n            .field(\"current\", &self.current)\n            .field(\"target\", &self.target)\n            .field(\"interval\", &self.interval)\n            .field(\"mode\", &self.mode)\n            .field(\"auto_start\", &self.auto_start)\n            .field(\"locale\", &self.locale)\n            .field(\"messages\", &self.messages)\n            .field(\"ids\", &self.ids)\n            .field(\"intl_backend\", &\"<dyn IntlBackend>\")\n            .finish()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl PartialEq for Context {\n    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {\n        self.current == other.current\n            && self.target == other.target\n            && self.interval == other.interval\n            && self.mode == other.mode\n            && self.auto_start == other.auto_start\n            && self.locale == other.locale\n            && self.messages == other.messages\n            && self.ids == other.ids\n    }\n}"
}

  "old_string": "    /// Progress as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.\n    ///\n    /// For countdown this is the fraction of [`Context::target`] already\n    /// elapsed; for stopwatch this is the elapsed time as a fraction of\n    /// [`Context::target`].\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (`HH:MM:SS` when hours are present, else `MM:SS`).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _millis) = self.display_time();\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\"{hours:02}:{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{minutes:02}:{seconds:02}\")\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Progress as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.\n    ///\n    /// For countdown this is the fraction of [`Context::target`] already\n    /// elapsed; for stopwatch this is the elapsed time as a fraction of\n    /// [`Context::target`]. The result is clamped to `[0.0, 1.0]` so an\n    /// over-target stopwatch or an out-of-range [`Event::SetTime`] never yields\n    /// a value outside the documented range (which would break the\n    /// `progressbar` `aria-valuenow`/`min`/`max` semantics).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        let progress = match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        };\n        progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0)\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (`HH:MM:SS` when hours are present, else `MM:SS`).\n    ///\n    /// Digits are rendered through [`Context::intl_backend`] so non-ASCII\n    /// numbering systems (e.g. Arabic-Indic) are honored when a localizing\n    /// backend is provided; the default [`StubIntlBackend`](ars_core::StubIntlBackend)\n    /// yields ASCII digits.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _millis) = self.display_time();\n        let format_segment = |value: u64| {\n            let width = NonZeroU8::new(2).expect(\"segment width is non-zero\");\n            u32::try_from(value).map_or_else(\n                |_| value.to_string(),\n                |value| {\n                    self.ctx\n                        .intl_backend\n                        .format_segment_digits(value, width, &self.ctx.locale)\n                },\n            )\n        };\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\n                \"{}:{}:{}\",\n                format_segment(hours),\n                format_segment(minutes),\n                format_segment(seconds)\n            )\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{}:{}\", format_segment(minutes), format_segment(seconds))\n        }\n    }"
}

The result is clamped to `[0.0, 1.0]` so an\n    /// over-target stopwatch or an out-of-range [`Event::SetTime`] never yields\n    /// a value outside the documented range (which would otherwise break the\n    /// `progressbar` `aria-valuenow`/`valuemin`/`valuemax` semantics).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n\n        let progress = match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        };\n\n        progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0)\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (`HH:MM:SS` when hours are present, else `MM:SS`).\n    ///\n    /// Digits are rendered through [`Context::intl_backend`] so non-ASCII\n    /// numbering systems (e.g. Arabic-Indic) are honored when a localizing\n    /// backend is provided; the default\n    /// [`StubIntlBackend`](ars_core::StubIntlBackend) yields ASCII digits.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _millis) = self.display_time();\n\n        let format_segment = |value: u64| {\n            let width = NonZeroU8::new(2).expect(\"segment width is non-zero\");\n            u32::try_from(value).map_or_else(\n                |_| value.to_string(),\n                |value| {\n                    self.ctx\n                        .intl_backend\n                        .format_segment_digits(value, width, &self.ctx.locale)\n                },\n            )\n        };\n\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\n                \"{}:{}:{}\",\n                format_segment(hours),\n                format_segment(minutes),\n                format_segment(seconds)\n            )\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{}:{}\", format_segment(minutes), format_segment(seconds))\n        }\n    }"
}
```

  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn timer_progress_clamped_to_unit_range() {\n        // Stopwatch past its target: raw fraction 2.0 clamps to 1.0.\n        let stopwatch = api_for(\n            State::Running,\n            Duration::from_secs(2),\n            Mode::Stopwatch,\n            Duration::from_secs(1),\n        );\n        assert!((stopwatch.progress() - 1.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n        assert_eq!(\n            stopwatch\n                .progress_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow)),\n            Some(\"100\")\n        );\n\n        // Countdown whose current exceeds target (e.g. via an out-of-range\n        // SetTime): raw -1.0 clamps to 0.0 instead of rendering \"-100\".\n        let countdown = api_for(\n            State::Running,\n            Duration::from_secs(2),\n            Mode::Countdown,\n            Duration::from_secs(1),\n        );\n        assert!((countdown.progress() - 0.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n        assert_eq!(\n            countdown\n                .progress_attrs()\n                .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow)),\n            Some(\"0\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_triggers_are_type_button() {\n        let api = api_for(\n            State::Running,\n            Duration::from_secs(30),\n            Mode::Countdown,\n            Duration::from_secs(60),\n        );\n\n        assert_eq!(api.start_trigger_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(\"button\"));\n        assert_eq!(api.pause_trigger_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(\"button\"));\n        assert_eq!(api.reset_trigger_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Type), Some(\"button\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_formatted_time_routes_digits_through_intl_backend() {\n        // 90s -> 01:30; the localizing backend prefixes each segment with `loc-`.\n        let api = api_with_localized_backend(Duration::from_secs(90));\n\n        assert_eq!(api.formatted_time(), \"loc-01:loc-30\");\n\n        // The root `aria-label` is sourced from `formatted_time`, so it inherits\n        // the localized digits too.\n        assert_eq!(\n            api.root_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label)),\n            Some(\"loc-01:loc-30\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    // ─────────────────────── snapshots ───────────────────────"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/spec/components/specialized/timer.md",
  "old_string": "    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0] (countdown only).\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() { return 0.0; }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS).\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (h, m, s, _) = self.display_time();\n        if h > 0 {\n            format!(\"{:02}:{:02}:{:02}\", h, m, s)\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{:02}:{:02}\", m, s)\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0].\n    ///\n    /// Clamped so an over-target stopwatch or out-of-range `SetTime` never\n    /// produces a value outside [0.0, 1.0] (which would break the progressbar\n    /// `aria-valuenow`/`valuemin`/`valuemax` semantics).\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() { return 0.0; }\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();\n        let progress = match self.ctx.mode {\n            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,\n            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,\n        };\n        progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0)\n    }\n\n    /// Formatted time string (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS).\n    ///\n    /// Digits are rendered through `intl_backend.format_segment_digits` so\n    /// non-ASCII numbering systems are honored; the colon separator is fixed.\n    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {\n        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _) = self.display_time();\n        let width = NonZeroU8::new(2).expect(\"segment width is non-zero\");\n        let segment = |value: u64| {\n            u32::try_from(value).map_or_else(\n                |_| value.to_string(),\n                |value| self.ctx.intl_backend.format_segment_digits(value, width, &self.ctx.locale),\n            )\n        };\n        if hours > 0 {\n            format!(\"{}:{}:{}\", segment(hours), segment(minutes), segment(seconds))\n        } else {\n            format!(\"{}:{}\", segment(minutes), segment(seconds))\n        }\n    }"
}

425	    pub fn start_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {

436	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);
437	        }

All three Codex findings from pass 1 are addressed, replied to, resolved, and pushed (commit `54c807f7`), with `xci-fast` green. Codex's second pass is now polling in the background.

Summary of what was fixed in response to Codex:
1. **Clamp `progress()` to `[0.0, 1.0]`** — over-target stopwatch / out-of-range `SetTime` no longer produce out-of-range `aria-valuenow`.
2. **`type="button"` on triggers** — prevents accidental form submission.
3. **Locale-aware digits** — `formatted_time()` (and the root `aria-label`) now route through `Env::intl_backend` per spec §4.1; `Context` carries `Arc<dyn IntlBackend>` like `TimeField`.

Each landed with a new test, spec sync, and refreshed snapshots. I'll report back when Codex returns its pass-2 verdict (👍 → done and ready for you to merge; or new threads → I address them the same way).

{

  "old_string": "    #[test]\n    fn timer_without_auto_start_emits_no_initial_effects() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.take_initial_effects().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn timer_without_auto_start_emits_no_initial_effects() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.take_initial_effects().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_context_eq_excludes_backend_and_debug_redacts_it() {\n        let props = test_props();\n        let (_, ctx) = Machine::init(&props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default());\n\n        // Equality ignores the injected backend (it is a service, not state):\n        // swapping the backend instance leaves the contexts equal.\n        let mut other_backend = ctx.clone();\n        other_backend.intl_backend = Arc::new(LocalizedDigitsBackend);\n        assert_eq!(ctx, other_backend);\n\n        // ...but a difference in observable state is detected.\n        let mut shifted = ctx.clone();\n        shifted.current = Duration::from_secs(5);\n        assert_ne!(ctx, shifted);\n\n        // Debug redacts the trait object rather than attempting to print it.\n        let debug = format!(\"{ctx:?}\");\n        assert!(debug.contains(\"timer::Context\"));\n        assert!(debug.contains(\"<dyn IntlBackend>\"));\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────"
}

> AGENT

Root cause found and fixed. Quick summary of what happened after Codex's 👍:

The CI **Coverage** job failed on the **CRAP gate** (complexity-vs-coverage) — which is *not* part of `xci-fast`, so my local pre-push runs couldn't surface it. The single regression: the manual `PartialEq for Context` impl I added (to carry the new `intl_backend` trait object) had **0% coverage** with cyclomatic complexity 8 → CRAP 72, flagged against `time_field`'s covered baseline.

Fix (commit `aeb077e8`): added `timer_context_eq_excludes_backend_and_debug_redacts_it`, which exercises `Context::eq` (equal path with backend swapped + unequal path) and the `Debug` redaction. That brings `Context::eq` to full coverage → CRAP drops 72 → 8 (well under the gate), and it asserts the documented "equality excludes the injected backend" contract. `xci-fast` green again.

I pushed and re-triggered `@codex review` (the new commit supersedes the prior 👍). I'm now polling both Codex's re-verdict and the full CI Coverage/CRAP check together. I'll report back when both land — Codex 👍 + green CI means it's ready for your merge, after which I'll close #294.

> AGENT

Codex's third pass found 3 new valid P2 edge-case findings (all legitimate, no design tension — I'll fix all):
- **A**: zero-length `interval` → non-terminating timer (clamp).
- **B**: `set_props` doesn't sync `target`/`interval`/`mode` into `Context` (no `on_props_changed` override) → transitions use stale values.
- **C**: zero-duration countdown should complete immediately, not run.

Let me read the current `init`/`transition` to implement precisely.

306	#[derive(Debug)]
307	pub struct Machine;
308	
309	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
310	    type State = State;
311	    type Event = Event;
312	    type Context = Context;
313	    type Props = Props;
314	    type Messages = Messages;
315	    type Effect = Effect;
316	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
317	
318	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
319	        let current = initial_duration(props.mode, props.target);
320	
321	        let state = if props.auto_start {
322	            State::Running
323	        } else {
324	            State::Idle
325	        };
326	
327	        (
328	            state,
329	            Context {
330	                current,
331	                target: props.target,
332	                interval: props.interval,
333	                mode: props.mode,
334	                auto_start: props.auto_start,
335	                locale: env.locale.clone(),
336	                messages: messages.clone(),
337	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
338	                intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),
339	            },
340	        )
341	    }
342	
343	    fn initial_effects(
344	        state: &Self::State,
345	        _ctx: &Self::Context,
346	        _props: &Self::Props,
347	    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {
348	        let mut effects = Vec::new();
349	
350	        // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a
351	        // transition, so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.
352	        if matches!(state, State::Running) {
353	            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));
354	        }
355	
356	        effects
357	    }
358	
359	    fn transition(
360	        state: &Self::State,
361	        event: &Self::Event,
362	        ctx: &Self::Context,
363	        _props: &Self::Props,
364	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
365	        match (state, event) {
366	            // Idle start, paused resume, and paused start all enter `Running`
367	            // and (re)emit the interval intent.
368	            (State::Idle, Event::Start) | (State::Paused, Event::Resume | Event::Start) => {
369	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running).with_effect(interval_effect()))
370	            }
371	
372	            (State::Running, Event::Tick) => match ctx.mode {
373	                Mode::Countdown => {
374	                    let new = ctx.current.saturating_sub(ctx.interval);
375	
376	                    if new.is_zero() {
377	                        Some(
378	                            TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)
379	                                .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
380	                                    ctx.current = Duration::ZERO;
381	                                })
382	                                .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
383	                                .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCompleted)),
384	                        )
385	                    } else {
386	                        Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
387	                            ctx.current = new;
388	                        }))
389	                    }
390	                }
391	                Mode::Stopwatch => {
392	                    let interval = ctx.interval;
393	                    Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
394	                        ctx.current = ctx.current.saturating_add(interval);
395	                    }))
396	                }
397	            },
398	
399	            (State::Running, Event::Pause) => {
400	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Paused).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))
401	            }
402	
403	            (_, Event::Reset) => {
404	                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);
405	                Some(
406	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
407	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
408	                            ctx.current = initial;
409	                        })
410	                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),
411	                )
412	            }
413	
414	            (_, Event::Restart) => {
415	                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);
416	                Some(
417	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)
418	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
419	                            ctx.current = initial;
420	                        })
421	                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
422	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)),
```

  "old_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        let current = initial_duration(props.mode, props.target);\n\n        let state = if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n        };\n\n        (\n            state,\n            Context {\n                current,\n                target: props.target,\n                interval: props.interval,\n                mode: props.mode,\n                auto_start: props.auto_start,\n                locale: env.locale.clone(),\n                messages: messages.clone(),\n                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n                intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),\n            },\n        )\n    }",
  "new_string": "    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        let current = initial_duration(props.mode, props.target);\n\n        // A zero-duration countdown is already complete; it must not enter\n        // `Running` (which would show `00:00` ticking and defer the\n        // announcement). `auto_start` is ignored in that degenerate case.\n        let state = if is_instantly_complete(props.mode, props.target) {\n            State::Completed\n        } else if props.auto_start {\n            State::Running\n        } else {\n            State::Idle\n        };\n\n        (\n            state,\n            Context {\n                current,\n                target: props.target,\n                // Guarantee forward progress: a zero interval would make every\n                // tick a no-op (countdown never reaches zero, stopwatch never\n                // advances) and keep the adapter interval alive forever.\n                interval: effective_interval(props.interval),\n                mode: props.mode,\n                auto_start: props.auto_start,\n                locale: env.locale.clone(),\n                messages: messages.clone(),\n                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n                intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),\n            },\n        )\n    }\n\n    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {\n        assert_eq!(\n            old.id, new.id,\n            \"timer::Props.id must remain stable after init\"\n        );\n\n        if old.target != new.target || old.interval != new.interval || old.mode != new.mode {\n            alloc::vec![Event::SyncProps]\n        } else {\n            Vec::new()\n        }\n    }"
}
