  "old_string": "            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current = initial;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)),\n                )\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(duration)) => {\n                let duration = *duration;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.current = duration;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n\n                // Restarting a zero-duration countdown completes immediately\n                // rather than entering `Running`; otherwise reset and run.\n                if is_instantly_complete(ctx.mode, ctx.target) {\n                    Some(\n                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                            .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                                ctx.current = initial;\n                            })\n                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),\n                    )\n                } else {\n                    Some(\n                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                            .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                                ctx.current = initial;\n                            })\n                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)),\n                    )\n                }\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(duration)) => {\n                let duration = *duration;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.current = duration;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SyncProps) => {\n                let target = props.target;\n                let interval = effective_interval(props.interval);\n                let mode = props.mode;\n                // While idle the displayed value mirrors the (new) initial; once\n                // running/paused/completed the in-progress `current` is kept.\n                let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle);\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.target = target;\n                    ctx.interval = interval;\n                    ctx.mode = mode;\n                    if reset_current {\n                        ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);\n                    }\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// The initial `current` value for a given mode and target.\nconst fn initial_duration(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Duration {\n    match mode {\n        Mode::Countdown => target,\n        Mode::Stopwatch => Duration::ZERO,\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds the marker effect that starts the adapter-owned tick interval.\nfn interval_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)\n}",
  "new_string": "/// The initial `current` value for a given mode and target.\nconst fn initial_duration(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Duration {\n    match mode {\n        Mode::Countdown => target,\n        Mode::Stopwatch => Duration::ZERO,\n    }\n}\n\n/// The smallest tick interval the machine will store.\n///\n/// A zero interval makes every tick a no-op (a countdown never reaches zero and\n/// a stopwatch never advances), so it is clamped up to 1ms to guarantee the\n/// timer always makes forward progress.\nconst MIN_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(1);\n\n/// Clamps a requested interval to [`MIN_INTERVAL`].\nconst fn effective_interval(interval: Duration) -> Duration {\n    if interval.as_nanos() < MIN_INTERVAL.as_nanos() {\n        MIN_INTERVAL\n    } else {\n        interval\n    }\n}\n\n/// Whether a `(mode, target)` pair describes a countdown that is already at\n/// zero and therefore complete on arrival.\nconst fn is_instantly_complete(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> bool {\n    matches!(mode, Mode::Countdown) && target.is_zero()\n}\n\n/// Builds the marker effect that starts the adapter-owned tick interval.\nfn interval_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)\n}"
}

Let me simplify `effective_interval` to use the precise `is_zero()` check (const-safe; only exact zero is non-terminating):

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01HZRmzzgKVhxKKJUAqfUm2h
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// The smallest tick interval the machine will store.\n///\n/// A zero interval makes every tick a no-op (a countdown never reaches zero and\n/// a stopwatch never advances), so it is clamped up to 1ms to guarantee the\n/// timer always makes forward progress.\nconst MIN_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(1);\n\n/// Clamps a requested interval to [`MIN_INTERVAL`].\nconst fn effective_interval(interval: Duration) -> Duration {\n    if interval.as_nanos() < MIN_INTERVAL.as_nanos() {\n        MIN_INTERVAL\n    } else {\n        interval\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// The fallback tick interval substituted for a zero interval.\n///\n/// A zero interval makes every tick a no-op (a countdown never reaches zero and\n/// a stopwatch never advances), so it is replaced with 1ms to guarantee the\n/// timer always makes forward progress.\nconst MIN_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(1);\n\n/// Replaces a zero interval with [`MIN_INTERVAL`]; any non-zero interval (even\n/// sub-millisecond) already makes progress and is left untouched.\nconst fn effective_interval(interval: Duration) -> Duration {\n    if interval.is_zero() {\n        MIN_INTERVAL\n    } else {\n        interval\n    }\n}"
}

zero-target stopwatch is not \"complete\" — it counts up from zero.\n        let stopwatch = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).target(Duration::ZERO));\n        assert_eq!(stopwatch.state(), &State::Idle);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_zero_target_restart_completes_immediately() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::ZERO));\n\n        let result = service.send(Event::Restart);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n        // No interval armed; it completed on arrival.\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_on_props_changed_emits_sync_only_for_context_backed_props() {\n        let base = test_props();\n\n      assert!(\n            <Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(\n                &base,\n                &Props {\n                    auto_start: true,\n                    ..base.clone()\n                },\n            )\n            .is_empty()\n        );\n\n        // target / interval / mode each trigger a sync.\n        for changed in [\n            Props {\n ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(&base, &changed),\n                vec![Event::SyncProps]\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_props_syncs_context_backed_props() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(60));\n\n        // Idle: synced fields land in context and `current` re-mirrors the target.\n        let result = service.set_props(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(30))\n                .interval(Duration::from_millis(500)),\n        );\n        assert!(result.context_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().target, Duration::from_secs(30));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_millis(500));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(30));\n\n        // A zero interval pushed via props is clamped on sync, too.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(30)).interval(Duration::ZERO)));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_millis(1));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_props_while_running_keeps_current_but_updates_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick)); // current -> 59s\n\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(60)).interval(Duration::from_secs(2))));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_secs(2));\n        // Mid-run elapsed value is preserved (not reset to the target).\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(59));\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────"

mode: Mode::Stopwatch,\n                ..base.clone()\n            },\n        ] {\n            assert_eq!(\n                <Machine as ars_core::Machine>::on_props_changed(&base, &changed),\n                vec![Event::SyncProps]\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_props_syncs_context_backed_props() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(60));\n\n        // Idle: synced fields land in context and `current` re-mirrors the target.\n        let result = service.set_props(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(30))\n                .interval(Duration::from_millis(500)),\n        );\n        assert!(result.context_changed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().target, Duration::from_secs(30));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_millis(500));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(30));\n\n        // A zero interval pushed via props is clamped on sync, too.\n        drop(\n            service.set_props(\n                test_props()\n                    .target(Duration::from_secs(30))\n                    .interval(Duration::ZERO),\n            ),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_millis(1));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_props_while_running_keeps_current_but_updates_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Tick)); // current -> 59s\n\n        drop(\n            service.set_props(\n                test_props()\n                    .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                    .interval(Duration::from_secs(2)),\n            ),\n        );\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Running);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().interval, Duration::from_secs(2));\n        // Mid-run elapsed value is preserved (not reset to the target).\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(59));\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────"
}
```

  "new_string": "            (_, Event::Restart) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n                // Restarting a zero-duration countdown completes immediately.\n                if is_instantly_complete(ctx.mode, ctx.target) {\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                        .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current = initial; })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n                } else {\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)\n                        .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current = initial; })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)))\n                }\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SetTime(duration)) => {\n                let duration = *duration;\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.current = duration;\n                }))\n            }\n\n            (_, Event::SyncProps) => {\n                let target = props.target;\n                let interval = effective_interval(props.interval);\n                let mode = props.mode;\n                // Idle mirrors the new initial; running/paused keep `current`.\n                let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle);\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.target = target;\n                    ctx.interval = interval;\n                    ctx.mode = mode;\n                    if reset_current {\n                        ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);\n                    }\n                }))\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }"

Codex pass 3's three edge-case findings are all addressed, replied, resolved, and pushed (`8c4d0362`), with `xci-fast` green. I re-triggered `@codex review` and am polling both Codex's verdict and the full CI (including the Coverage/CRAP gate) together.

What landed this round:
- **Zero `interval`** → clamped to a 1ms floor (`effective_interval`), so ticks always make progress.
- **Controlled prop changes** → new `Event::SyncProps` + `on_props_changed` sync `target`/`interval`/`mode` into `Context` (and re-mirror `current` when idle), so transitions never use stale values.
- **Zero-`target` countdown** → initializes/restarts as `Completed` instead of running `00:00`; stopwatches unaffected.

Each with new tests and spec sync (§1.2/§1.6). I'll report back when both Codex and CI return — 👍 + green means ready for your merge, then I close #294.

reset to the target).\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(59));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_set_props_interval_change_while_running_rearms_interval() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),\n        );\n        drop(service.send(Event::Start));\n\n        // Changing the cadence mid-run cancels and re-emits the adapter\n        // interval so it restarts at the new rate.\n        let changed = service.set_props(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(60))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(2)),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(changed.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&changed), vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        // A sync that does not change the cadence does not churn the interval.\n        let unchanged = service.set_props(\n            test_props()\n                .target(Duration::from_secs(30))\n                .interval(Duration::from_secs(2)),\n        );\n        assert!(unchanged.cancel_effects.is_empty());\n        assert!(unchanged.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_zero_target_reset_stays_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::ZERO));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n\n        // Reset must not drop a zero-duration countdown back to a startable Idle.\n        drop(service.send(Event::Reset));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_sync_into_zero_target_countdown_completes() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(60)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n\n        // Updating props to a zero-duration countdown completes on sync rather\n        // than leaving an idle, startable zero timer.\n        let result = service.set_props(\n            test_props()\n                .mode(Mode::Countdown)\n                .target(Duration::ZERO),\n        );\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────"

  "new_string": "            (_, Event::Reset) => {\n                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);\n                // A zero-duration countdown resets to Completed, not Idle, so it\n                // cannot be (re)started into a running 00:00.\n                let target_state = if is_instantly_complete(ctx.mode, ctx.target) {\n                    State::Completed\n                } else {\n                    State::Idle\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(target_state)\n                    .apply(move |ctx| { ctx.current = initial; })\n                    .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n            }"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-294/spec/components/specialized/timer.md",
  "old_string": "            (_, Event::SyncProps) => {\n                let target = props.target;\n                let interval = effective_interval(props.interval);\n                let mode = props.mode;\n                // Idle mirrors the new initial; running/paused keep `current`.\n                let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle);\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.target = target;\n                    ctx.interval = interval;\n                    ctx.mode = mode;\n                    if reset_current {\n                        ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);\n                    }\n                }))\n            }",
  "new_string": "            (_, Event::SyncProps) => {\n                let target = props.target;\n                let interval = effective_interval(props.interval);\n                let mode = props.mode;\n                // Syncing into a zero-duration countdown completes on arrival.\n                if is_instantly_complete(mode, target) {\n                    Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                        .apply(move |ctx| {\n                            ctx.target = target;\n                            ctx.interval = interval;\n                            ctx.mode = mode;\n                            ctx.current = Duration::ZERO;\n                        })\n                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval))\n                } else {\n                    // Idle mirrors the new initial; running/paused keep `current`.\n                    let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle);\n                    // A live cadence change re-arms the adapter interval.\n                    let rearm = matches!(state, State::Running) && interval != ctx.interval;\n                    let mut plan = TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {\n                        ctx.target = target;\n                        ctx.interval = interval;\n                        ctx.mode = mode;\n                        if reset_current {\n                            ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);\n                        }\n                    });\n                    if rearm {\n                        plan = plan\n                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));\n                    }\n                    Some(plan)\n                }\n            }"
}

Codex pass 4's three consistency findings are addressed, replied, resolved, and pushed (`550f4109`) with `xci-fast` green. Re-triggered `@codex review`; polling Codex + CI together.

This round closed the gaps my pass-3 zero-duration logic left open:
- Mid-run `interval` change now **re-arms** the adapter interval (cancel + re-emit).
- Zero-target countdown stays **Completed** on `Reset` (not a startable `Idle`).
- Syncing props into a zero-duration countdown transitions to **Completed**.

The zero-duration-countdown invariant is now uniform across init/start/reset/restart/sync. I'll report back when Codex + CI return their verdicts.

  "new_string": "    #[must_use]\n    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {\n        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() {\n            // A zero-duration countdown is complete on arrival, so it reports\n            // full progress; a zero-target stopwatch has no meaningful target\n            // and is reported as indeterminate (0.0).\n            return if self.is_completed() { 1.0 } else { 0.0 };\n        }\n\n        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();"
}

  "old_string": "    /// Root element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"timer\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_str())\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), \"polite\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), \"true\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.formatted_time());\n\n        attrs\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Root element attributes.\n    ///\n    /// The Root is the polite, atomic `aria-live` region. It deliberately does\n    /// **not** carry the formatted time as an `aria-label`: live regions\n    /// announce changes to their *content* (the [`Display`](Part::Display) text),\n    /// not to `aria-label`, so labelling the region with the live value would\n    /// not be announced and would shadow the visible text. Associate an\n    /// accessible name via the optional [`Label`](Part::Label) part.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"timer\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_str())\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Live), \"polite\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Atomic), \"true\");\n\n        attrs\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_sync_from_completed_to_live_target_returns_to_idle() {\n        // A finished zero-duration countdown reconfigured to a runnable target\n        // must leave Completed so it is startable again (not stranded).\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::ZERO));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n\n        let result = service.set_props(test_props().target(Duration::from_secs(30)));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().current, Duration::from_secs(30));\n        assert_eq!(result.cancel_effects, vec![Effect::TimerInterval]);\n\n        // Switching a completed timer to stopwatch likewise frees it from Completed.\n        let mut sw = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::ZERO));\n        drop(sw.set_props(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).target(Duration::from_secs(30))));\n        assert_eq!(sw.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert_eq!(sw.context().current, Duration::ZERO);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn timer_completed_zero_target_reports_full_progress() {\n        // A zero-duration countdown sits in Completed; its progress must read as\n        // 100% (not 0%) so the progressbar reflects completion.\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().target(Duration::ZERO));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n\n        let api = service.connect(&|_| {});\n        assert!((api.progress() - 1.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n        assert_eq!(\n            api.progress_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueNow)),\n            Some(\"100\")\n        );\n\n        // A zero-target stopwatch has no meaningful target and stays indeterminate.\n        let stopwatch = fresh_service(test_props().mode(Mode::Stopwatch).target(Duration::ZERO));\n        let sw_api = stopwatch.connect(&|_| {});\n        assert!((sw_api.progress() - 0.0).abs() < 1e-9);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────"
}

  "new_string": "                } else {\n                    // Reconfiguring a completed timer to a runnable config must\n                    // leave Completed, else it stays completed/disabled (or a\n                    // stopwatch stuck in Completed) until manual reset.\n                    let was_completed = matches!(state, State::Completed);\n                    // Idle and (newly-runnable) Completed mirror the new initial;\n                    // running/paused keep the in-progress `current`.\n                    let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle | State::Completed);\n                    // A live cadence change re-arms the adapter interval.\n                    let rearm = matches!(state, State::Running) && interval != ctx.interval;\n                    let apply = move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.target = target;\n                        ctx.interval = interval;\n                        ctx.mode = mode;\n                        if reset_current {\n                            ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);\n                        }\n                    };\n                    let mut plan = if was_completed {\n                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(apply).cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                    } else {\n                        TransitionPlan::context_only(apply)\n                    };\n                    if rearm {\n                        plan = plan\n                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)\n                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));\n                    }\n                    Some(plan)\n                }\n            }"

358	        state: &'a Self::State,

367	/// The initial `current` value for a given mode and target.
368	const fn initial_duration(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Duration {
369	    match mode {
370	        Mode::Countdown => target,
371	        Mode::Stopwatch => Duration::ZERO,
372	    }
373	}
374	
375	/// Replaces a zero interval with a 1ms floor so ticks always make progress.
376	const fn effective_interval(interval: Duration) -> Duration {
377	    if interval.is_zero() { Duration::from_millis(1) } else { interval }
378	}
379	
380	/// Whether a `(mode, target)` describes a countdown that is already complete.
381	const fn is_instantly_complete(mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> bool {
382	    matches!(mode, Mode::Countdown) && target.is_zero()
383	}
384	```

401	
402	pub struct Api<'a> {
403	    state: &'a State,
404	    ctx: &'a Context,
405	    props: &'a Props,
406	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
407	}
408	
409	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
410	    pub fn is_running(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Running }
411	    pub fn is_paused(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Paused }
412	    pub fn is_completed(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Completed }
413	    pub fn is_idle(&self) -> bool { *self.state == State::Idle }
414	    pub fn current(&self) -> Duration { self.ctx.current }
415	
416	    /// Current time broken into hours, minutes, seconds, milliseconds.
417	    pub fn display_time(&self) -> (u64, u64, u64, u64) {
418	        let ms = self.ctx.current.as_millis() as u64;
419	        let hours = ms / 3_600_000;
420	        let minutes = (ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000;
421	        let seconds = (ms % 60_000) / 1_000;
422	        let millis = ms % 1_000;
423	        (hours, minutes, seconds, millis)
424	    }
425	
426	    /// Progress as a fraction [0.0, 1.0].
427	    ///
428	    /// Clamped so an over-target stopwatch or out-of-range `SetTime` never
429	    /// produces a value outside [0.0, 1.0] (which would break the progressbar
430	    /// `aria-valuenow`/`valuemin`/`valuemax` semantics).
431	    pub fn progress(&self) -> f64 {
432	        if self.ctx.target.is_zero() { return 0.0; }
433	        let fraction = self.ctx.current.as_secs_f64() / self.ctx.target.as_secs_f64();
434	        let progress = match self.ctx.mode {
435	            Mode::Countdown => 1.0 - fraction,
436	            Mode::Stopwatch => fraction,
437	        };
438	        progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
439	    }
440	
441	    /// Formatted time string (HH:MM:SS or MM:SS).
442	    ///
443	    /// Digits are rendered through `intl_backend.format_segment_digits` so
444	    /// non-ASCII numbering systems are honored; the colon separator is fixed.
445	    pub fn formatted_time(&self) -> String {
446	        let (hours, minutes, seconds, _) = self.display_time();
447	        let width = NonZeroU8::new(2).expect("segment width is non-zero");
448	        let segment = |value: u64| {
449	            u32::try_from(value).map_or_else(
450	                |_| value.to_string(),
451	                |value| self.ctx.intl_backend.format_segment_digits(value, width, &self.ctx.locale),
452	            )
453	        };
454	        if hours > 0 {
455	            format!("{}:{}:{}", segment(hours), segment(minutes), segment(seconds))
456	        } else {
457	            format!("{}:{}", segment(minutes), segment(seconds))
458	        }
459	    }
460

635	
636	| Part         | Role          | Properties                                               |

645	
646	| Key           | Action                  |
647	| ------------- | ----------------------- |
648	| Enter / Space | Activate focused button |
649	| Tab           | Move between controls   |
650	
651	### 3.3 Screen Reader Announcements
652	
653	The Root element has `role="timer"` with `aria-live="polite"` and `aria-atomic="true"`. Time changes are announced periodically. When countdown completes, the `completed_announcement` message is announced.

The Root no longer carries the time as\n   `aria-label` (live regions announce content, not label changes); accessible\n   name is associated via the optional `Label` part.\n\nTests: sync-from-completed→idle (countdown + stopwatch), completed zero progress\n= 100%, Display not aria-hidden, Root has no time label. Spec §1.6/§1.7/§2/§3.1/\n§3.3 synced; root/display snapshots refreshed.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG\nsleep 12\ngit log --oneline -1 | cat\ngit push 2>&1 | tail -3",
